| [REQ-3128](REQ-3128-task-text-size.md) | Task text MUST be at least 24 CSS pixels high in the normal text size. | approved |
| [REQ-3130](REQ-3130-large-story-text-size.md) | When the player turns on large text, story text MUST be at least 24 CSS pixels high. | approved |
| [REQ-3132](REQ-3132-large-task-text-size.md) | When the player turns on large text, task text MUST be at least 28 CSS pixels high. | approved |
| [REQ-3134](REQ-3134-fonts-cover-cyrillic.md) | Every font the game uses MUST draw the whole Russian alphabet, «ё» included, without falling back to another font. | approved |
| [REQ-3136](REQ-3136-equal-width-digits.md) | Digits in answers, keypad keys and counters MUST all have the same width. | approved |
| [REQ-3138](REQ-3138-no-black-or-grey-shadows.md) | Shadows in the interface MUST NOT be black or neutral grey. | approved |
| [REQ-3140](REQ-3140-element-colour-fixed.md) | Each element's colour MUST stay the same in every palette. | approved |
| [REQ-3142](REQ-3142-parent-state-colours-fixed.md) | The colours of skill states in the Parent Room MUST NOT change with the player's palette. | approved |
| [REQ-3144](REQ-3144-no-emoji.md) | The interface MUST NOT use emoji for icons or labels. | approved |
| [REQ-3146](REQ-3146-no-fast-flashing.md) | The game MUST NOT show anything that flashes more than three times in any one second. | approved |
| [REQ-3148](REQ-3148-no-sudden-loud-sound.md) | The game MUST NOT play a sudden loud sound. | approved |
| [REQ-3200](REQ-3200-no-tick-or-cross.md) | The player's screens MUST NOT show a tick or a cross to mark an answer or a result. | approved |
| [REQ-3202](REQ-3202-system-window-announced.md) | When a System window appears, a screen reader MUST announce it without interrupting what it is reading. | approved |
| [REQ-3204](REQ-3204-keypad-key-size.md) | Every key of the maths keypad MUST be at least 64 CSS pixels in both directions. | approved |
| [REQ-3206](REQ-3206-keypad-on-the-right.md) | On the tablet the maths keypad MUST sit on the right side of the screen, under the player's thumb. | approved |
| [REQ-3208](REQ-3208-keypad-fixed-layout.md) | Each key of the maths keypad MUST keep the same position in every task, and a key a task doesn't use leaves its cell empty. | approved |
| [REQ-3210](REQ-3210-physical-keyboard-answers.md) | When a physical keyboard is attached, the answer field MUST accept typed digits and signs as the on-screen keypad would. | approved |
| [REQ-3212](REQ-3212-options-not-coloured.md) | After a multiple-choice answer, the game MUST NOT colour any option as right or wrong. | approved |
| [REQ-3214](REQ-3214-outcome-icon-and-word.md) | Every outcome MUST show an icon and a word together. | approved |
| [REQ-3216](REQ-3216-scheme-shows-her-answer.md) | The knot scheme MUST show the player's own answer beside the steps the code builds. | approved |
| [REQ-3218](REQ-3218-xp-bar-no-daily-progress.md) | The experience bar MUST NOT show progress through the day. | approved |
| [REQ-3220](REQ-3220-element-shown-whole.md) | An element MUST always appear with its colour, its icon and its name together. | approved |
| [REQ-3222](REQ-3222-element-shows-her-name.md) | When the player has given an element her own name, every place that shows the element MUST show her name in place of the default one. | approved |
| [REQ-3224](REQ-3224-counters-named-to-readers.md) | Every resource counter MUST name its resource to a screen reader. | approved |
| [REQ-3226](REQ-3226-chest-quality-in-words.md) | Each reward in a chest MUST state its quality in words. | approved |
| [REQ-3228](REQ-3228-unmet-creature-name-hidden.md) | A creature the player hasn't met MUST show «???» in place of its name. | approved |
| [REQ-3230](REQ-3230-story-lines-announced.md) | When a new line appears in the story log, a screen reader MUST announce it without interrupting what it is reading. | approved |
| [REQ-3232](REQ-3232-story-speakers-distinct.md) | The story log MUST give the narrator, characters, familiars, the heroine, the Diary and System windows each a look of its own. | approved |
| [REQ-3234](REQ-3234-story-voice-input.md) | The story input MUST offer voice input beside the text field. | approved |
| [REQ-3236](REQ-3236-keys-pick-suggestion.md) | When the player presses 1, 2 or 3 on a physical keyboard while the story input's text field is empty, the game MUST pick the matching suggestion. | superseded |
| [REQ-3238](REQ-3238-keys-select-option.md) | When the player presses 1, 2, 3 or 4 on a physical keyboard during a multiple-choice task, the game MUST select the matching option. | approved |
| [REQ-3240](REQ-3240-quests-never-failed.md) | The game MUST NOT mark an unfinished quest as failed. | approved |
| [REQ-3242](REQ-3242-palette-choice-preview.md) | Each palette on offer MUST preview its own colours in the current theme. | approved |
| [REQ-3244](REQ-3244-custom-colour-any-pick.md) | Each colour role of a custom palette MUST accept any colour the player picks, from the offered swatches or from the device's colour picker. | approved |
| [REQ-3246](REQ-3246-task-window-follows-looks.md) | The task window MUST follow the player's chosen theme and palette. | approved |
| [REQ-3300](REQ-3300-system-lines-short-formal.md) | Every System line MUST be short, formal and in the present tense. | approved |
| [REQ-3302](REQ-3302-system-no-exclamation.md) | A System line MUST NOT contain an exclamation mark. | approved |
| [REQ-3304](REQ-3304-self-correction-separate.md) | When the System corrects itself, the correction MUST appear as a line of its own after a pause. | approved |
| [REQ-3306](REQ-3306-system-marks-events.md) | A System line MUST describe an event in the world, and never the heroine's mind or abilities. | approved |
| [REQ-3308](REQ-3308-system-numbers-from-code.md) | Every number in a System window MUST come from a field the code fills, and never from the text of the line. | approved |
| [REQ-3310](REQ-3310-world-labels.md) | The interface MUST use the world's labels: «Схема узла» (the knot scheme) and «Как легла нить» (how the thread lay) for the review, «Твоё заклинание» (your spell) for her answer, «Готово» (Done), «Не знаю» (I don't know) and «Путеводная нить» (guiding thread) for the task buttons, «Распутан начисто» (untangled cleanly), «Почти чисто» (nearly clean), «Узел ослаблен» (the knot is loosened) and «Принято» (accepted) for outcomes, «Привал» (rest stop), «Сохранить и уйти» (Save and leave) and «Ещё один ряд» (One more row) for leaving, and «Дней в Башне» (Days in the Tower) for the day counter. | approved |
| [REQ-3312](REQ-3312-no-rejected-labels.md) | An interface label MUST NOT read «Правильный ответ» (the correct answer), «Решение» (the solution), «Твой ответ» (your answer), «Проверить» (Check), «Подсказка» (Hint), «Сдаться» (Give up), «Верно» (correct), «Выйти» (Exit), «Пауза» (Pause), «Осталось N минут» (N minutes left), «Серия» (Streak) or «Дней подряд» (Days in a row). | approved |
| [REQ-3314](REQ-3314-forbidden-list.md) | Text the heroine sees MUST NOT contain, in any inflected form, «неправильно», «неверно», «ошибка», «ошиблась», «промах», «мимо», «провал», «жаль», «не получилось», «не смогла», «плохо», «неудача», «ты не поняла», «это же просто», «задача», «пример», «урок», «школа», «оценка», «умница», «гений», «ты умная» or «ты самая умная»; only her own words shown back as she wrote them and the Parent Room are exempt. | approved |
| [REQ-3316](REQ-3316-no-guilt-phrases.md) | Text the heroine sees MUST NOT contain a phrase of guilt or attachment, such as «я скучал, почему ты не приходила» (I missed you, why didn't you come) or «Ты нас подвела» (You let us down). | approved |
| [REQ-3318](REQ-3318-jokes-never-at-heroine.md) | A joke in the game's text MUST target the world, the System, the Tangles or the Guardians, and never the heroine. | approved |
| [REQ-3320](REQ-3320-knot-never-uzelok.md) | Text the heroine sees MUST NOT call a knot «узелок». | approved |
| [REQ-3322](REQ-3322-thread-button-label.md) | The guiding thread button MUST read «Путеводная нить · N» (guiding thread · N), with N the current thread count filled by code. | approved |
| [REQ-3324](REQ-3324-no-running-out-of-threads.md) | Text the heroine sees MUST NOT tell her that she has run out of guiding threads. | approved |
| [REQ-3326](REQ-3326-forbidden-check-every-source.md) | Every line the heroine sees MUST pass an automatic check against the forbidden list before she sees it, whether the line comes from the Master, the line pool, the Explainer, a task template or the interface. | approved |
| [REQ-3328](REQ-3328-one-forbidden-list.md) | Every forbidden-list check MUST read the same single list, so a word added once is blocked in every kind of text. | approved |
| [REQ-3330](REQ-3330-ally-named-bantik.md) | From the autumn chapter's finale on, every text MUST name the ally Mister Knot becomes «Бантик» (Little Bow), and never «Узелок» (Little Knot). | approved |
| [REQ-3400](REQ-3400-one-art-style.md) | Every picture in the game MUST follow one style: soft pastel patisserie anime in the manner of a visual novel, with a thin warm brown line. | approved |
| [REQ-3402](REQ-3402-no-pure-black-in-art.md) | Pictures in the game MUST NOT use pure black, in lines or in fills. | approved |
| [REQ-3404](REQ-3404-heroine-matches-sheet.md) | Every picture of the heroine MUST show her as a catgirl matching the character sheet the family chose, or keyart-heroine until the family has chosen. | approved |
| [REQ-3406](REQ-3406-cloak-colour-tints-cardigan.md) | The cloak colour the player chooses MUST tint only the heroine's cardigan and bows. | approved |
| [REQ-3408](REQ-3408-child-proportions-covered.md) | Every character MUST be drawn with child proportions and covered clothes: shoulders covered, hems at the knee or longer, no maid costume and no suggestive pose. | approved |
| [REQ-3410](REQ-3410-creatures-round-plush.md) | Familiars, Tangles and Guardians MUST be drawn round and plush, with a silhouette that reads at first glance. | approved |
| [REQ-3412](REQ-3412-no-text-in-art.md) | Art assets MUST NOT contain text in any language. | approved |
| [REQ-3414](REQ-3414-icon-drawing-style.md) | Every icon and element symbol MUST be the game's own flat drawing, with a warm brown outline, pastel fills and a white highlight. | approved |
| [REQ-3416](REQ-3416-cloak-is-the-cardigan.md) | «Плащ охотницы» (the huntress's cloak) MUST be drawn as the heroine's hooded cable-knit cardigan, with knitted ears on the hood and a heart patch on the sleeve, and never as a second garment over it. | approved |
| [REQ-3418](REQ-3418-pugovka-look.md) | «Пуговка» (Little Button) MUST be drawn honey-coloured, with four holes that spill sparks. | approved |
| [REQ-3420](REQ-3420-no-grey-mouse.md) | Pictures of the heroine MUST NOT show a grey mouse in her backpack. | approved |
| [REQ-3422](REQ-3422-heroine-sheets-vary-little.md) | The four heroine character sheets MUST differ only in hair colour and style, eye colour and the cardigan's colour, and keep the catgirl ears and tail, the cardigan and the dress silhouette the same. | approved |
| [REQ-3500](REQ-3500-story-text-at-centre.md) | Every story screen MUST give its centre to the story log and the input line, with the scene picture in a side column. | approved |
| [REQ-3502](REQ-3502-scene-picture-no-buttons.md) | The scene picture on a story screen MUST hold no buttons. | approved |
| [REQ-3504](REQ-3504-save-and-leave-visible.md) | Every story screen MUST show «Сохранить и уйти» (Save and leave). | approved |
| [REQ-3506](REQ-3506-settings-visible.md) | Every story screen MUST show the way into the player's settings. | approved |
| [REQ-3508](REQ-3508-task-window-no-animation.md) | The task window MUST NOT animate anything. | approved |
| [REQ-3510](REQ-3510-thread-button-beside-done.md) | The task window MUST place the guiding thread button beside «Готово» (Done). | approved |
| [REQ-3512](REQ-3512-guardian-starts-with-model.md) | When a Guardian sets a word problem, the problem MUST open with a choice of one short model from four before the step-by-step input. | superseded |
| [REQ-3514](REQ-3514-underside-scared-button.md) | Every Underside screen MUST show the «Мне страшно» (I'm scared) button. | approved |
| [REQ-3516](REQ-3516-underside-shows-light.md) | Every Underside screen MUST show a light. | approved |
| [REQ-3518](REQ-3518-test-mode-leaves-record.md) | Play in the parent's test mode MUST NOT change the player's record, except for the parent's notes and ambiguous-task marks. | superseded |
| [REQ-3520](REQ-3520-test-mode-marked.md) | Every test-mode screen MUST show a frame and a label that mark it as test mode. | superseded |
| [REQ-3522](REQ-3522-text-size-choice.md) | The player's settings MUST let the player choose normal or large text at any time. | approved |
| [REQ-3524](REQ-3524-sound-choice.md) | The player's settings MUST let the player turn sound on or off at any time. | superseded |
| [REQ-3526](REQ-3526-creepiness-heading.md) | The parent's creepiness setting MUST carry the heading «Уровень жуткости» (Creepiness level). | approved |
| [REQ-3528](REQ-3528-awakening-closing-line.md) | The Awakening's closing System line MUST read «Пробуждение завершено. Добро пожаловать в Башню.» (Awakening complete. Welcome to the Tower.) | approved |
| [REQ-3530](REQ-3530-awakening-rank-badge.md) | At the Awakening's closing line, the rank badge beside the System window MUST show rank E. | approved |
| [REQ-3532](REQ-3532-hatch-no-move-name.md) | The hatching screen MUST NOT suggest a move's name as a familiar's name. | approved |
| [REQ-3534](REQ-3534-hatch-no-used-name.md) | The hatching screen MUST NOT suggest a name already in use in the player's game. | approved |
| [REQ-3600](REQ-3600-llm-text-safety-check.md) | Every text a language model writes MUST pass the automatic safety check before the player sees it. | approved |
| [REQ-3602](REQ-3602-task-text-placeholder-frames-only.md) | Language-model text MUST enter a task's text only as a story frame whose numbers are placeholders that code fills. | approved |
| [REQ-3604](REQ-3604-library-frames-for-probes-guardian-fallback.md) | Probes, the Guardian ladder (лестница Стражей) and fallback cases MUST take their story frames from the approved frame library. | approved |
| [REQ-3606](REQ-3606-library-five-frames-per-structure.md) | The frame library MUST hold at least 5 frames for every word-problem structure by stage 0.2. | approved |
| [REQ-3608](REQ-3608-library-twenty-frames-per-structure.md) | The frame library MUST hold at least 20 frames for every word-problem structure by stage 0.4. | approved |
| [REQ-3610](REQ-3610-frame-no-repeat-fortnight.md) | The game MUST NOT show the player a story frame it showed her in the last 14 days while the structure has a frame it hasn't. | approved |
| [REQ-3612](REQ-3612-frame-repeat-longest-ago.md) | When a structure has no story frame left unshown for 14 days, the game MUST use the frame it showed longest ago. | approved |
| [REQ-3614](REQ-3614-frame-repeat-logged.md) | When the game shows a story frame again within 14 days, the event log MUST mark the repeat. | approved |
| [REQ-3616](REQ-3616-live-frames-top-up-only.md) | The game MUST NOT use a live story frame except for a task added to complete a node's full block. | approved |
| [REQ-3618](REQ-3618-live-frame-queue-ahead.md) | While live generation is on, the game MUST hold checked live frames for the next 2 to 3 rooms before the player reaches them. | approved |
| [REQ-3620](REQ-3620-frame-request-specification.md) | Every request for a story frame MUST give the author model the problem's steps, the role of each number, the floor, the characters in the scene and the limits on length and vocabulary. | approved |
| [REQ-3622](REQ-3622-frame-variants-per-request.md) | Every request for a story frame MUST ask the author model for 5 variants. | approved |
| [REQ-3624](REQ-3624-frame-reply-schema.md) | The game MUST discard a frame author's reply that doesn't conform to the fixed reply schema. | approved |
| [REQ-3626](REQ-3626-frame-placeholders-exactly-once.md) | The game MUST reject a story frame in which any placeholder is missing or appears more than once. | approved |
| [REQ-3628](REQ-3628-frame-no-other-numbers.md) | The game MUST reject a story frame that holds any number besides its placeholders, in digits or in words, such as «два» (two), «половина» (half) or «дюжина» (dozen). | approved |
| [REQ-3630](REQ-3630-frame-three-blind-solves.md) | The game MUST accept a story frame only when a checking model, solving it blind with each of three sets of numbers, gets the engine's answer all three times. | approved |
| [REQ-3632](REQ-3632-live-frame-final-blind-solve.md) | A live story frame MUST pass one more blind solve with the task's actual numbers before the player sees it. | approved |
| [REQ-3634](REQ-3634-blind-solver-sees-no-answer.md) | The blind solve of a story frame MUST NOT show the checking model the engine's answer or any list of candidate answers. | approved |
| [REQ-3636](REQ-3636-offline-frame-review-actions.md) | The Parent Room MUST let the parent accept, reject or edit each offline story frame in the review queue. | approved |
| [REQ-3638](REQ-3638-library-holds-accepted-frames.md) | The frame library MUST hold only frames the parent accepted, as written or as edited. | approved |
| [REQ-3640](REQ-3640-live-generation-off-on-high-rejection.md) | When more than 30% of the live frames checked on a game day are rejected, the game MUST stop generating live frames until the day ends and take frames from the library. | approved |
| [REQ-3642](REQ-3642-live-frames-kept-with-status.md) | The game MUST keep every live story frame with its check status. | approved |
| [REQ-3644](REQ-3644-live-frame-to-library.md) | The Parent Room MUST let the parent move a live story frame into the frame library with one action. | approved |
| [REQ-3646](REQ-3646-llm-calls-recorded.md) | The game MUST record every language-model request and its response. | approved |
| [REQ-3648](REQ-3648-science-from-bank-only.md) | Natural science questions MUST come only from the hand-made science bank. | superseded |
| [REQ-3650](REQ-3650-science-parent-approval.md) | A natural science question MUST reach the player only after the parent approves that question. | approved |
| [REQ-3652](REQ-3652-science-repeat-window-small-bank.md) | Before stage 0.5, the game MUST NOT repeat a natural science question within 45 days while its topic has a question not shown in that time. | approved |
| [REQ-3654](REQ-3654-science-repeat-window-large-bank.md) | From stage 0.5, the game MUST NOT repeat a natural science question within 90 days while its topic has a question not shown in that time. | approved |
| [REQ-3656](REQ-3656-science-repeat-longest-ago.md) | When a topic has no fresh natural science question, the game MUST use the question it showed longest ago. | approved |
| [REQ-3658](REQ-3658-science-repeat-logged.md) | When the game repeats a natural science question within its no-repeat window, the event log MUST mark the repeat. | approved |
| [REQ-3660](REQ-3660-science-only-from-approved-bank.md) | Natural science questions MUST come only from the science bank, never from text generated during play. | approved |
| [REQ-3700](REQ-3700-first-version-includes-mvp-parts.md) | The first version MUST include every part of the MVP contents list: levels and experience with daily quests; chests with a choice of 1 from 3, the forge and the buttons-only shop; the MVP familiar roster with friendship and evolution; the Master's story with success and other-path branches, floor states, Diary pages, names the player gives, free text and light dreamcore; the single mode with guiding threads, explanations, the event log and knowledge model v1; and, for the parent, lesson marks, report v1 and the raw data export. | superseded |
| [REQ-3702](REQ-3702-first-version-excludes-deferred-items.md) | The first version MUST NOT contain any item the draft defers until after the MVP: Ascents and anchor forms; story battles between familiars and the ring of elements; items and familiars made by AI, and live pictures; Diary ciphers; the free mode «Свободная прогулка» (Free Walk); characteristics, paths and story ranks by the calendar; room decor; the Dutch layer; and the full roster of familiars with the full set of dreamcore. | superseded |
| [REQ-3704](REQ-3704-player-facing-text-in-russian.md) | Every text and task the player sees in the first version MUST be in Russian. | superseded |
| [REQ-3706](REQ-3706-game-teaches-no-new-topics.md) | The game MUST NOT offer lessons that teach a new maths topic. | superseded |
| [REQ-3708](REQ-3708-canon-agrees-with-specification.md) | The canon MUST NOT contradict the specification on method, time, rewards or safety. | approved |
| [REQ-3710](REQ-3710-parent-sets-age-and-group.md) | The Parent Room MUST let the parent set the player's real name, age and school group, and change each at any time. | approved |
| [REQ-3712](REQ-3712-rules-read-configured-age-and-group.md) | Every rule that depends on the player's real name, age or school group MUST read the values the parent set, never a value fixed in the code, the content or a tracked file. | approved |
| [REQ-3714](REQ-3714-game-named-meowtower.md) | The game, its repository and its technical names MUST carry the name Meowtower («Мяубашня» in the player's language). | approved |
| [REQ-3800](REQ-3800-event-identity-and-order.md) | Every event MUST carry a unique identifier, a sequence number that places it in one increasing order over the whole log, its event type and the version of its payload schema. | approved |
| [REQ-3802](REQ-3802-derived-data-records-versions.md) | Every derived table and snapshot MUST record the model, threshold and graph versions it was computed with, the sequence number of the last event it took into account and the time it was computed. | approved |
| [REQ-3804](REQ-3804-shown-task-records-answer.md) | When the game shows a task, the event log MUST record its correct answer and its short solution as they would be shown. | approved |
| [REQ-3808](REQ-3808-task-parameters-language-free.md) | A task's parameters MUST NOT depend on the display language. | approved |
| [REQ-3810](REQ-3810-strings-in-language-files.md) | Every player-facing string MUST come from a per-language file outside the code. | approved |
| [REQ-3812](REQ-3812-graph-overlay-keeps-base-graph.md) | A curriculum overlay on the skill graph MUST add nodes and links without changing any node or link of the base graph. | approved |
| [REQ-3814](REQ-3814-report-keeps-layers-apart.md) | The report MUST NOT mix results from different graph layers unless the parent chooses to mix them. | approved |
| [REQ-3816](REQ-3816-explanation-cache-holds-no-fact.md) | Emptying the explanation cache MUST lose no fact about play. | approved |
| [REQ-3910](REQ-3910-judge-answers-only-the-mac.md) | A judge model running on the parent's Mac MUST give no model answer to a request from any device other than that Mac. | approved |
| [REQ-3912](REQ-3912-judge-answer-carries-probabilities.md) | The server MUST treat a local judge's answer to a check as an error unless it names one of the check's fixed answers and gives a probability, between 0 and 1, for each of them. | approved |
| [REQ-3914](REQ-3914-local-judge-meets-timeout.md) | A check MUST move to a judge model on the Mac only after that judge answers the check's test set on the family Mac, with the check's fixed prompt already cached and as many checks running at once as the game sends in one turn, within the judge timeout ADR-0100 sets, now 1500 ms, at the 95th percentile, measured at the gateway. | approved |
| [REQ-3916](REQ-3916-judge-chosen-per-check.md) | Among the models on the Mac that pass a judge check's agreement test (REQ-1688) and latency test (REQ-3914), the one with the highest agreement with the reference model MUST answer the check; models within one percentage point of each other tie, and a tie goes to the model that answers the most checks, then to the smaller model. | approved |
| [REQ-3918](REQ-3918-judge-supports-russian.md) | A model on the Mac whose model card lists its supported languages without Russian MUST NOT answer a judge check. | approved |
| [REQ-3920](REQ-3920-changed-judge-retested.md) | When the model file a judge on the Mac serves has a different hash from the one a check passed on, the server MUST NOT send that judge the check until the check's agreement test (REQ-1688) and latency test (REQ-3914) have passed on the file it serves. | approved |
| [REQ-3922](REQ-3922-startup-confirms-local-judge.md) | When the server starts, it MUST confirm that each judge on the Mac answers, and read the hash of the model file it serves, before it sends that judge a check. | approved |
| [REQ-3924](REQ-3924-unpassed-check-stays-hosted.md) | A judge check for which no model on the Mac passes both its agreement test (REQ-1688) and its latency test (REQ-3914) MUST stay with the hosted judge if that judge passed the check's test set, and otherwise with the safety model, as the approved record places it (RES-1600, RES-3910). | approved |
| [REQ-5000](REQ-5000-game-day-ends-at-four-device-zone.md) | The game day MUST end at 04:00 in the time zone of the device the player plays on. | approved |
| [REQ-5002](REQ-5002-day-boundary-never-shown.md) | The game MUST NOT show the player the hour at which the game day ends, on any screen, in any line or as any number. | approved |
| [REQ-5004](REQ-5004-one-new-adventure-per-game-day.md) | The game MUST start at most one new adventure in a game day. | approved |
| [REQ-5006](REQ-5006-day-turn-told-as-reknitting.md) | When a new game day begins, the story MUST present the change of day as «Башня перевязалась за ночь» (The Tower re-knitted itself overnight). | approved |
| [REQ-5008](REQ-5008-daily-jobs-run-at-day-end.md) | At the end of each game day, the game MUST run each of these daily jobs: the morning's guiding threads, the daily quests and daily rewards, the shop's new slots, the reset of the explanation budget, the end of a pause of live frames, the count of adventure days for the three-day rule, the end of «Закончить на сегодня» (Finish for today), the end of the frontier cap after a second anxiety signal, the end of the lowered creepiness level and the soft stop's daily reset. | approved |
| [REQ-5010](REQ-5010-nothing-opens-by-time-of-day.md) | The game MUST NOT open or close any screen, form or feature because of the time of day. | approved |
| [REQ-5012](REQ-5012-taskless-screens-whenever-task-window-closed.md) | Whenever the task window is closed, during an adventure included, the game MUST let the player reach the heroine's room, the Diary, the forge, the shop, the familiars, the puzzle branch «Петельки Смотрителя» (the Keeper's Loops) and «Свободное перо» (Free Pen). | approved |
| [REQ-5014](REQ-5014-new-puzzle-only-after-finale.md) | The game MUST add a new puzzle only after the day's finale. | approved |
| [REQ-5016](REQ-5016-taskless-screen-time-counts-for-eyes.md) | Time on the screens without tasks MUST count towards the 20-minute count for the next eye exercise, whenever she spends it, and time on a puzzle open at a rest stop included. | approved |
| [REQ-5018](REQ-5018-daily-quests-every-game-day.md) | Once daily quests and daily rewards have first appeared, the game MUST offer them on every game day on which the player plays. | approved |
| [REQ-5020](REQ-5020-day-routes-add-to-quests.md) | The choice of two routes of the day MUST come in addition to the daily quests, never in their place. | approved |
| [REQ-5022](REQ-5022-clock-check-passes-task-content.md) | The check that no player screen shows a clock MUST fail on every display of her own current or elapsed time and on nothing printed inside a task, such as a timetable or a clock face to read. | approved |
| [REQ-5024](REQ-5024-new-form-writes-own-stream.md) | Every form of task the owner's addendum 1 adds, such as a composed riddle, a solution plan, an estimate, a grouping or a task with Dutch keywords, MUST write its observations to a stream of its own. | approved |
| [REQ-5026](REQ-5026-new-stream-out-of-own-estimate.md) | A new form's stream MUST NOT enter the "on her own" estimate until a model version that admits it predicts held-out unassisted first attempts with lower log-loss and lower calibration error than the active version. | approved |
| [REQ-5028](REQ-5028-surplus-missing-subtypes-are-new-forms.md) | The word-problem subtypes with a surplus or a missing number MUST write a stream of their own, which stays out of their node's "on her own" estimate under the rule for new forms in REQ-5026. | approved |
| [REQ-5030](REQ-5030-new-model-job-own-role.md) | Every use of a language model that the owner's addendum 1 adds MUST run under a model role of its own. | approved |
| [REQ-5032](REQ-5032-new-role-output-checked.md) | The game MUST use the output of every model role the owner's addendum 1 adds only after that output has passed a check against the role's declared output format and the data rules that bind the role, and otherwise use the role's fallback. | approved |
| [REQ-5034](REQ-5034-offline-framing-on-offline-key.md) | The framing of hint rungs in the familiar's voice and the retelling of puzzles MUST spend from the offline key, under an offline role and never under the planner's play role. | approved |
| [REQ-5036](REQ-5036-parse-request-masks-numbers.md) | A request to parse a composed riddle MUST carry the riddle with every number replaced by a placeholder, so that the numbers stay on the Mac. | approved |
| [REQ-5038](REQ-5038-parse-request-no-target.md) | A request to parse a composed riddle MUST NOT carry the task's target expression. | approved |
| [REQ-5040](REQ-5040-parser-keeps-nothing.md) | The server MUST send a request to parse a composed riddle only to a provider configured to keep no part of the request after it answers. | approved |
| [REQ-5042](REQ-5042-data-leaving-the-mac.md) | The server MUST NOT send off the parent's Mac any data other than content made without the player, the player's story material (her cleaned free text, invented names, story memory and summary outcome events), the age the parent set, the one-task explanation request and a composed riddle with every number masked. | approved |
| [REQ-5044](REQ-5044-parent-told-what-leaves.md) | The Parent Room MUST tell the parent the five kinds of data that leave the Mac: content made without the player, the player's story material (her cleaned free text, invented names, story memory and summary outcome events), the age the parent set, the one-task explanation request and her composed riddles with every number masked. | approved |
| [REQ-5046](REQ-5046-daily-parse-budget.md) | The spend on parsing composed riddles MUST stop at a parse budget of $0.1 per game day (REQ-5000), counted on the play key. | approved |
| [REQ-5048](REQ-5048-play-key-daily-caps-under-monthly-limit.md) | The daily budgets that spend from the play key MUST sum, over a 31-day month, to less than the play key's $60 monthly limit. | approved |
| [REQ-5050](REQ-5050-sandbox-spends-offline-key.md) | The model features of the parent's sandbox and the agent's command-line sandbox MUST spend from the offline key and never from the play key. | approved |
| [REQ-5052](REQ-5052-sandbox-monthly-cap.md) | The spend of the parent's sandbox and the agent's command-line sandbox MUST stop at a monthly cap of its own on the offline key. | approved |
| [REQ-5054](REQ-5054-free-pen-spends-adventure-leftover.md) | «Свободное перо» (Free Pen) MUST spend only what the day's adventure budget has left, and never a budget of its own. | approved |
| [REQ-5056](REQ-5056-free-pen-closes-by-story-scene.md) | When the day's adventure budget runs out during «Свободное перо» (Free Pen), the game MUST close it through a story scene. | approved |
| [REQ-5058](REQ-5058-school-goals-stay-on-mac.md) | The server MUST NOT send the school's goal list, or any part of it, off the Mac. | approved |
| [REQ-5060](REQ-5060-parent-confirms-goal-links.md) | A link between a school goal and a node MUST take effect only after the parent confirms it. | approved |
| [REQ-5062](REQ-5062-event-type-needs-owner-and-schema.md) | The server MUST NOT write an event of a type that lacks an owning decision or a payload schema. | approved |
| [REQ-5064](REQ-5064-event-names-vendor-neutral.md) | Event types and code MUST NOT name the vendor of the school's learning system. | approved |
| [REQ-5066](REQ-5066-changed-payload-new-version.md) | A change to the fields of `attempt_submitted`, `item_shown` or `verdict` MUST arrive as a new version of that type's payload schema that still reads the events stored under earlier versions. | approved |
| [REQ-5068](REQ-5068-hint-shown-per-rung.md) | A `hint_shown` event MUST record each hint rung shown to the player, whether or not a guiding thread paid for that rung. | approved |
| [REQ-5070](REQ-5070-thread-spent-on-ladder-or-explanation.md) | A `thread_spent` event MUST record each guiding thread the player spends, on opening a task's hint ladder or on an explanation. | approved |
| [REQ-5072](REQ-5072-one-record-per-fact.md) | The event log MUST record each estimate, grouping, plan choice and self-check in one place only. | approved |
| [REQ-5074](REQ-5074-log-records-parent-content-acts.md) | The event log MUST record the parent's approval of a puzzle, the parent's approval of a hint rung's framing, each time a template or a puzzle is turned off, and the parent's mark «тренировали факты» (we trained facts). | approved |
| [REQ-5076](REQ-5076-first-version-includes-mvp-parts.md) | The first version MUST include every part of the MVP contents list: levels and experience with daily quests; chests with a choice of 1 from 3, the forge and the buttons-only shop; the MVP familiar roster with friendship and evolution; the Master's story with success and other-path branches, floor states, Diary pages, names the player gives, free text and light dreamcore; the single mode with guiding threads, explanations, the event log and knowledge model v1; for the parent, lesson marks, report v1 and the raw data export; and the addendum's items 1 to 9 and 11 to 13: the hint ladder, composing a word problem, the estimate with the inverse check, word problems with a surplus or a missing number, rational grouping, solution plan cards, the puzzle branch «Петельки Смотрителя» (the Keeper's Loops), preparation for the M7 and E7 tests with fact automaticity, the Sources track «Карта Смотрителя» (the Keeper's Map) with its nodes I1 to I5, its report section and its source component, silent play, the rules that keep the game the player's own, and the parent's sandbox. | approved |
| [REQ-5078](REQ-5078-first-version-excludes-deferred-items.md) | The first version MUST NOT contain any item deferred until after the MVP: Ascents and anchor forms; story battles between familiars and the ring of elements; items and familiars made by AI, and live pictures; Diary ciphers, though not the puzzle branch «Петельки Смотрителя» (the Keeper's Loops); the free mode «Свободная прогулка» (Free Walk); characteristics, paths and story ranks by the calendar; room decor; the Dutch layer; the full roster of familiars with the full set of dreamcore; and snapshots of the school's learning system. | approved |
| [REQ-5080](REQ-5080-player-text-russian-except-bridge.md) | Every text and task the player sees in the first version MUST be in Russian, apart from the Dutch keywords of the word bridge and the Dutch equivalent a term hint shows, each once the parent has approved it. | approved |
| [REQ-5082](REQ-5082-bridge-holds-thirty-to-fifty-words.md) | The Dutch word bridge MUST hold 30 to 50 keywords. | approved |
| [REQ-5084](REQ-5084-bridge-in-fifth-of-tasks.md) | The Dutch keywords of the word bridge MUST appear in 15 % to 25 % of the T1 to T4 and Sources tasks shown to the player over any 14 game days on which she plays. | approved |
| [REQ-5086](REQ-5086-bridge-word-parent-approval.md) | The game MUST NOT show a Dutch keyword of the word bridge before the parent approves that word through the glossary's review. | approved |
| [REQ-5088](REQ-5088-fact-stage-before-mvp.md) | The player MUST be able to play a fact-measurement stage, with the facts of the M7 and E7 preparation and the hint ladder on the stage 0.1 templates, as soon as a person accepts that stage and before stage 0.3. | approved |
| [REQ-5090](REQ-5090-addendum-build-order.md) | The build MUST take the addendum's items in this order: fact measurement with the hint ladder first; then the estimate with the inverse check, word problems with a surplus or a missing number, solution plan cards and the Sources track; then composing a word problem and rational grouping. | approved |
| [REQ-5092](REQ-5092-sandbox-from-stage-zero-one.md) | The parent's sandbox MUST be available from stage 0.1, with the first templates. | approved |
| [REQ-5094](REQ-5094-sandbox-model-features-wait.md) | The parent's sandbox MUST NOT offer a model feature until the model gateway and the Parent Room's PIN exist. | approved |
| [REQ-5096](REQ-5096-compose-waits-for-parser-test.md) | Until the masked parser has matched the labelled verdicts on at least 95 % of 200 reference riddles in a live run on the offline key, and whenever the game day's parse budget has run out, «Сплети загадку» (Weave a riddle) MUST offer sentence cards in place of free composition. | approved |
| [REQ-5098](REQ-5098-sandbox-closed-to-player-before-pin.md) | Until the Parent Room's PIN guards the parent's sandbox, the game MUST NOT let the player reach the sandbox from her own screens. | approved |
| [REQ-5100](REQ-5100-ladder-opening-costs-one-thread.md) | When the player opens the hint ladder on a task with the thread button before answering, the opening MUST cost exactly 1 guiding thread. | approved |
| [REQ-5102](REQ-5102-further-rungs-cost-nothing.md) | Once the player has opened the hint ladder on a task, every further rung on that task MUST cost no guiding thread. | approved |
| [REQ-5104](REQ-5104-twin-ladder-costs-own-thread.md) | Opening the hint ladder on the parallel task of a second attempt MUST cost 1 guiding thread of its own, whether or not the player opened the ladder on the first task. | approved |
| [REQ-5106](REQ-5106-ladder-length-matches-real-steps.md) | The hint ladder of every task that offers one before the answer MUST have one rung for each real step of its template, up to three rungs, with no rung written only to make up the count. | approved |
| [REQ-5108](REQ-5108-solution-rungs-explanation-agree.md) | A task's short solution, each of its hint rungs and its per-trap template explanations MUST use the same steps and the same numbers. | approved |
| [REQ-5110](REQ-5110-basic-fact-one-strategy-rung.md) | The hint ladder of a basic fact that offers one before the answer MUST have exactly one rung, which gives a strategy through a known fact, such as «7 · 8 — это 7 · 7 и ещё 7» (7 x 8 is 7 x 7 and 7 more). | approved |
| [REQ-5112](REQ-5112-first-rung-numbers-only-in-facts.md) | The first hint rung MUST contain no numbers unless it is the strategy rung of a basic fact. | approved |
| [REQ-5114](REQ-5114-fluent-fact-no-ladder-before-answer.md) | When a task is a basic fact in mental arithmetic whose fluency threshold is 10 seconds or less, the game MUST NOT offer a hint ladder before the answer. | approved |
| [REQ-5116](REQ-5116-fluent-fact-strategy-after-answer.md) | When the player answers a basic fact in mental arithmetic whose fluency threshold is 10 seconds or less, the game MUST include its strategy hint in the short solution, whether the task window shows that solution at once after a miss or offers it after a right answer. | approved |
| [REQ-5118](REQ-5118-early-rungs-never-hold-answer.md) | Rungs 1 and 2 of every template MUST NOT state the task's correct answer as a result, over 1000 seeds of that template. | approved |
| [REQ-5120](REQ-5120-task-window-holds-controls-and-familiar.md) | The task window MUST hold only the task, the answer field or options, the keypad, «Не знаю» (I don't know), the guiding thread button and «Готово» (Done), with no sprite, effect or story text, except the texts and controls a task's own form adds: a shown hint rung with the familiar's portrait and framing line beside it, the short solution and a fluent fact's strategy hint after the answer, the estimate's four options and «Проверить нить» (Check the thread) with its check field and match signal, «Нельзя узнать» (Can't be known) with its four options on a T1 to T4 word problem, a riddle's text field with its starters, paraphrase line and two confirmation buttons, a grouping task's tappable numbers and the loops they draw, and a drawn source with its tappable regions. | approved |
| [REQ-5122](REQ-5122-familiar-frames-each-rung.md) | When the task window shows a hint rung before the answer, including a rung restored on resume, it MUST show beside it the portrait of the familiar accompanying the player and one approved framing line for that familiar's kind and that rung, because the addendum makes the familiar the one who gives the help. | approved |
| [REQ-5124](REQ-5124-framing-carries-no-task-content.md) | A familiar's framing line MUST carry no number, step, operation or reference to the parts of a task. | approved |
| [REQ-5126](REQ-5126-framing-with-digit-rejected.md) | The game MUST reject, before it reaches the review queue, a framing line that contains a digit or a Russian cardinal or ordinal numeral in any inflection, «один» and «одна» included. | approved |
| [REQ-5128](REQ-5128-framing-shown-after-approval.md) | The game MUST NOT show a framing line to the player before the parent approves it in the review queue. | approved |
| [REQ-5130](REQ-5130-twin-after-miss-or-deep-hint.md) | When a first attempt ends with a wrong answer or «Не знаю» (I don't know), or the player saw a rung 2 or rung 3 hint on it whatever its outcome, the game MUST give one second attempt on a parallel task as REQ-5166 defines it. | approved |
| [REQ-5132](REQ-5132-no-twin-after-partial-without-deep-hint.md) | When a first attempt ends with a partial answer («почти», almost) and the player saw no hint rung beyond rung 1, the game MUST NOT give a second attempt. | approved |
| [REQ-5134](REQ-5134-no-twin-after-right-with-rung-one.md) | When a first attempt ends with a right answer and the player saw no hint rung beyond rung 1, the game MUST NOT give a second attempt. | approved |
| [REQ-5136](REQ-5136-with-help-kept-by-depth.md) | The knowledge model MUST keep, for each node and subtype, the share of right answers after rung 1, after rung 2, after rung 3 and on the second attempt, as separate figures. | approved |
| [REQ-5138](REQ-5138-depth-shares-half-life.md) | Each share of right answers by depth of help MUST weight an assisted attempt by 2^(-age in days / 30). | approved |
| [REQ-5140](REQ-5140-pooled-with-help-kept.md) | The knowledge model MUST keep one "with help" estimate pooled over every depth of help beside the shares by depth. | approved |
| [REQ-5142](REQ-5142-first-model-ignores-depth.md) | The first version of the knowledge model MUST NOT use the depth of help in any estimate other than the shares by depth; the report's marks and figures that read the depth aren't estimates. | approved |
| [REQ-5144](REQ-5144-depth-reading-model-must-predict-better.md) | A knowledge model version that reads the depth of help MUST NOT replace the first version unless it predicts the next unassisted first attempt with lower log-loss and lower calibration error on held-out days, the activation gate of the knowledge model's decision. | approved |
| [REQ-5146](REQ-5146-on-threshold-mark.md) | The report MUST mark a node «на пороге» (on the threshold) when its state is «Пока не освоено» (not mastered yet) or «Уточняется» (being clarified), and at least 60 % of at least 3 assisted attempts within the last 14 days were right after rung 1. | approved |
| [REQ-5148](REQ-5148-on-threshold-before-nearly-ready.md) | When a node meets the rules for both «на пороге» (on the threshold) and «почти готово» (nearly ready), the report MUST show both marks, with «на пороге» first. | approved |
| [REQ-5150](REQ-5150-mean-depth-of-help.md) | The report MUST show, for each node beside «решает с подсказкой» (solves with a hint), the mean depth of help over the node's attempts in the last 30 days, counting a first attempt as 0 with no hint or as its deepest rung from 1 to 3, and a second attempt as 4 whatever hint it used. | approved |
| [REQ-5152](REQ-5152-log-records-what-paid-for-rung.md) | When the game shows a hint rung, the event log MUST record it as `thread` if its request spent the guiding thread and as `free_step` if it was shown with no charge, so the log tells a paid rung from a free one. | approved |
| [REQ-5154](REQ-5154-log-records-ladder-spending.md) | When opening a hint ladder spends a guiding thread, the event log MUST record the spending with the reason `hint_ladder`. | approved |
| [REQ-5156](REQ-5156-log-records-ladder-length.md) | When the player submits an attempt, the event log MUST record the number of rungs the task's hint ladder has, beside the deepest rung she saw. | approved |
| [REQ-5158](REQ-5158-resume-restores-open-ladder.md) | When the player resumes a task whose hint ladder she opened, the game MUST show the ladder open, with the rungs she already saw. | approved |
| [REQ-5160](REQ-5160-resume-reopens-ladder-free.md) | When the player resumes a task whose hint ladder she opened, the game MUST NOT spend a guiding thread to reopen that ladder. | approved |
| [REQ-5162](REQ-5162-fluent-fact-strategy-costs-nothing.md) | The strategy hint a fluent basic fact shows after the answer MUST cost no guiding thread. | approved |
| [REQ-5164](REQ-5164-long-template-ladder-groups-steps.md) | When a template has more than three real steps, its hint ladder MUST group them into three rungs. | approved |
| [REQ-5166](REQ-5166-twin-matches-first-task.md) | The parallel task of a second attempt MUST have the same template, subtype and difficulty features as the first attempt's task, except that the twin of a problem with a missing number is drawn at random as a missing-number or a solvable problem of the same tier, so that over 1000 seeds both kinds occur. | approved |
| [REQ-5168](REQ-5168-resume-logs-no-restored-rung.md) | When a resume restores a hint rung the player already saw, the event log MUST NOT record that rung as shown again. | approved |
| [REQ-5200](REQ-5200-parse-request-carries-no-target.md) | A parse request MUST carry only the fixed parse prompt and the player's cleaned composed text, and no target expression, node id, topic name, problem type, verdict or other answer. | approved |
| [REQ-5202](REQ-5202-numbers-masked-before-parse.md) | Before a parse request leaves the Mac, the engine MUST replace every number in the player's composed text, digit runs and Russian number words alike, with a token n1 to nm in text order. | approved |
| [REQ-5204](REQ-5204-number-mapping-stays-on-mac.md) | The mapping from the tokens n1 to nm back to the numbers in the player's composed text MUST stay on the Mac. | approved |
| [REQ-5206](REQ-5206-parse-names-only-tokens.md) | The engine MUST reject as invalid any parse whose graph names a number other than one of the tokens of the masked text it was sent. | approved |
| [REQ-5208](REQ-5208-parse-player-tier-zero-retention.md) | Every parse request MUST go only to an endpoint that keeps no copy of the request after answering it, at a provider on the player-tier list. | approved |
| [REQ-5210](REQ-5210-startup-checks-parse-model.md) | The server MUST refuse to start when the parse model has no zero-retention endpoint at a provider on the player-tier list. | approved |
| [REQ-5212](REQ-5212-judge-may-carry-composed-text.md) | A request to the judge model MUST carry only the cleaned text its question needs, which for a composed riddle is the riddle's text with every number masked as REQ-5202 masks it, and no story memory, outcome events, times, estimates, other answers or other maths result. | approved |
| [REQ-5214](REQ-5214-parse-calls-logged.md) | The gateway MUST record every parse call in the log of model calls. | approved |
| [REQ-5216](REQ-5216-parse-budget-per-game-day.md) | Parse calls MUST spend on the play key from a parse budget of $0.1 a game day and from no other budget. | approved |
| [REQ-5218](REQ-5218-riddle-only-when-parse-can-run.md) | The Director MUST offer a riddle only on a floor that holds word problems, and only when the parse budget can still reserve two worst-case parses, the gateway's live calls are on, the composing flag is on and the parent hasn't switched off the free-text field. | approved |
| [REQ-5220](REQ-5220-composed-text-safety-before-parse.md) | Before any parse request leaves the Mac, the player's composed text MUST pass the same local triggers as story free text on the raw text, and then the judge's safety check on the cleaned, masked text. | approved |
| [REQ-5222](REQ-5222-composed-serious-trigger-path.md) | When the player's composed text trips a serious trigger or a serious signal, the game MUST take the same serious path as for story free text. | approved |
| [REQ-5224](REQ-5224-compose-verdict-from-engine.md) | The verdict on a composed riddle MUST depend only on the target and the graph the player confirmed, and give the same verdict each time for the same pair. | approved |
| [REQ-5226](REQ-5226-compose-match-rule.md) | The engine MUST give a confirmed graph the verdict `match` when it uses the same operations on the same numbers as the target, counting `+` and `·` as commutative and associative and keeping `−` and `:` in their fixed order. | approved |
| [REQ-5228](REQ-5228-compose-other-structure-rule.md) | The engine MUST give a confirmed graph the verdict `match_other_structure` when its operations differ from the target's and its value equals the target's. | approved |
| [REQ-5230](REQ-5230-compose-wrong-numbers-rule.md) | The engine MUST give the verdict `wrong_structure` to a confirmed graph that uses the target's operations on numbers other than the target's. | approved |
| [REQ-5232](REQ-5232-compose-wrong-numbers-marked.md) | When a riddle gets `wrong_structure` because its graph uses the target's operations on other numbers, the log MUST mark the verdict as that case. | approved |
| [REQ-5234](REQ-5234-paraphrase-only-templates-and-her-spans.md) | The familiar's paraphrase of a parse MUST hold only per-language template strings and spans that the engine found, word for word, in the player's own text. | approved |
| [REQ-5236](REQ-5236-rejected-correction-ends-unparsed.md) | When the player rejects the paraphrase of her corrected text, or her corrected text fails to parse, the riddle MUST end as `unparsed`, with base experience and no observation. | approved |
| [REQ-5238](REQ-5238-corrected-text-counts-unassisted.md) | A riddle the player corrects after rejecting the paraphrase MUST count as unassisted. | approved |
| [REQ-5240](REQ-5240-corrected-riddle-log-keeps-both.md) | When the player corrects a riddle, the log MUST mark the attempt as corrected and keep both her first and her corrected text. | approved |
| [REQ-5242](REQ-5242-compose-verdicts-own-field.md) | The verdicts and error classes of a composed riddle MUST be recorded only in the `compose_parsed` and `compose_confirmed` events, in a verdict field that no other attempt uses. | approved |
| [REQ-5244](REQ-5244-compose-error-classes-conceptual.md) | The error-type limit MUST place `compose_on_vs_times`, `compose_partition_vs_quotition` and `compose_order` in the conceptual class. | approved |
| [REQ-5246](REQ-5246-riddle-not-an-observation.md) | A riddle attempt MUST NOT count as an observation for the "with help" or fluency estimates, a block, a probe, a node state or the success share the Director reads before a room slot, until ADR-0060's activation rule admits the composing stream. | approved |
| [REQ-5248](REQ-5248-holding-steps-excludes-riddles.md) | Every unassisted first attempt on a k-step word problem in daily play, from a Guardian or from a room, MUST count towards step k of the holding-steps limit unless it is a rapid guess, the parent excluded it, it is a riddle the player composed, or it is a problem with a missing number. | approved |
| [REQ-5250](REQ-5250-step-input-share-excludes-riddles.md) | Between 40% and 60% of the compound word problems (tiers T2 to T4) the player gets to solve in any 30 days MUST take step-by-step input, and the rest the final answer only, with riddles she composes counted in neither part. | approved |
| [REQ-5252](REQ-5252-composing-stream-counts.md) | The composing stream MUST keep, for each tier and problem type, the count of each riddle verdict other than `unparsed`. | approved |
| [REQ-5254](REQ-5254-can-compose-report-line.md) | The report line «Может составить задачу» (Can compose a problem) MUST show the composing stream's counts beside the matrix of type by steps, and no state drawn from them. | approved |
| [REQ-5256](REQ-5256-director-reads-tier-node-state.md) | Until a record defines when the player solves a problem type at least at "understands", the Director MUST offer a riddle of a type only when the node of that type's tier is at least at "understands". | approved |
| [REQ-5258](REQ-5258-riddle-daily-cap.md) | The Director MUST offer at most 2 riddles a game day. | approved |
| [REQ-5260](REQ-5260-riddle-same-flow-scored-or-not.md) | Every riddle the game shows MUST run the same compose flow, whether or not it is scored. | approved |
| [REQ-5262](REQ-5262-wrong-structure-no-twin.md) | When a riddle gets `wrong_structure`, it MUST end with the short review and no twin riddle. | approved |
| [REQ-5264](REQ-5264-riddle-outside-room-slots.md) | A riddle MUST play as a story scene outside the room slots, so it adds nothing to the room's length. | approved |
| [REQ-5266](REQ-5266-riddle-strings-per-language.md) | The riddle's field label, its three starter buttons, the paraphrase templates and the two confirmation buttons MUST each come from the per-language content files. | approved |
| [REQ-5268](REQ-5268-free-text-points-include-riddle.md) | The free-text field MUST open only on entering a floor, before a Guardian, at the campfire on a rest stop, at the session finale, on meeting a new creature, at a riddle, and in «Свободное перо» (Free Pen) once it has opened, unless the parent switched it off. | approved |
| [REQ-5270](REQ-5270-diary-riddle-pages-from-log.md) | The Diary pages «Загадки героини» (The heroine's riddles) MUST show each riddle's text as the log holds it, with no model writing any part of the page. | approved |
| [REQ-5272](REQ-5272-composed-text-not-to-master.md) | Text the player composed for a riddle MUST NOT enter a request to the Master or the Master's story memory. | approved |
| [REQ-5274](REQ-5274-parse-test-masked-offline.md) | Acceptance test 3 MUST run the parser on masked text, in the live verification run, on the offline key. | approved |
| [REQ-5276](REQ-5276-parse-test-labels-before-run.md) | Acceptance test 3 MUST use reference texts that the parent labels by the engine's verdict rules before the parser runs on them. | approved |
| [REQ-5278](REQ-5278-parse-test-set-contents.md) | The reference set of acceptance test 3 MUST hold 200 texts, among them child-like spelling errors, dictated text, number words, problems with no question, irrelevant data, every verdict, every compose error class and correct problems in each allowed order of operands. | approved |
| [REQ-5280](REQ-5280-parse-test-confusion-report.md) | Acceptance test 3 MUST report how often each labelled verdict was judged as each verdict, beside its agreement of at least 95%. | approved |
| [REQ-5282](REQ-5282-parse-test-misblame-threshold.md) | Acceptance test 3 MUST fail when more than 2% of the reference texts labelled `match` are judged other than `match`. | approved |
| [REQ-5284](REQ-5284-parse-test-request-bodies.md) | Acceptance test 3 MUST check, from the log of model calls, that every parse request body holds exactly the fixed parse prompt and the masked text of its riddle. | approved |
| [REQ-5286](REQ-5286-parse-test-planted-target.md) | Acceptance test 3 MUST plant a distinctive target expression in a fixture and find it in no logged parse request. | approved |
| [REQ-5288](REQ-5288-parse-failure-gives-unparsed.md) | When a parse times out after 10 seconds, returns invalid JSON or returns a graph with no question, the riddle MUST get the verdict `unparsed`, with base experience and no observation. | approved |
| [REQ-5290](REQ-5290-composing-flag-needs-pass.md) | The composing flag MUST stay off unless acceptance test 3 has passed on masked text on the parse model the gateway is configured with. | approved |
| [REQ-5292](REQ-5292-sentence-cards-fallback.md) | If the masked parse can't pass acceptance test 3, the composing form MUST be built as sentence cards the engine judges, with no model. | approved |
| [REQ-5294](REQ-5294-parent-reads-riddles.md) | The Parent Room MUST show the parent every riddle's composed text, both texts of a corrected riddle, the paraphrase, the player's answer to it and the verdict. | approved |
| [REQ-5296](REQ-5296-owner-sees-rejected-share.md) | The Parent Room MUST show the share of paraphrases the player rejected over the last 30 days. | approved |
| [REQ-5298](REQ-5298-compose-other-value-wrong.md) | The engine MUST give the verdict `wrong_structure` to a confirmed graph whose value differs from the target's. | approved |
| [REQ-5300](REQ-5300-estimate-before-exact-answer.md) | The game MUST ask for an estimate before the exact answer on between 10 % and 20 % of the scored tasks, counted over at least 200 such tasks, of multi-digit multiplication and division, decimals, percentages, area and volume, and T2 to T4 word problems whose correct result is 1000 or more. | approved |
| [REQ-5302](REQ-5302-estimate-once-per-room.md) | The game MUST ask for at most one estimate in each room. | approved |
| [REQ-5304](REQ-5304-estimate-on-unscored-tasks-too.md) | The game MUST ask for an estimate on the unscored tasks of the subtypes that carry one, warm-ups and easy tasks included, at the same rate as on their scored tasks. | approved |
| [REQ-5306](REQ-5306-no-estimate-verdict-before-exact.md) | The server MUST NOT send the verdict on an estimate before the player submits the exact answer of the same task. | approved |
| [REQ-5308](REQ-5308-estimate-and-exact-verdicts-together.md) | The game MUST show the verdict on an estimate together with the verdict on the exact answer of the same task. | approved |
| [REQ-5310](REQ-5310-outcome-from-exact-answer-alone.md) | A task's outcome, its change to the streak and its rewards MUST depend on the exact answer alone and never on the estimate. | approved |
| [REQ-5312](REQ-5312-estimate-offers-four-rounded-values.md) | The estimate MUST offer four rounded values of different orders of magnitude as separate buttons, and never intervals on a number line. | approved |
| [REQ-5314](REQ-5314-estimate-correct-option-even-positions.md) | Over the estimates of each subtype, the correct option MUST fall on each of the four positions equally often, within what chance allows. | approved |
| [REQ-5316](REQ-5316-estimate-errors-of-order-off-correct.md) | The value ten times the correct result, the value one tenth of it and, when the task's final operation is multiplication, the sum of its operands MUST each fall on an option other than the correct one. | approved |
| [REQ-5318](REQ-5318-estimate-feeds-number-sense-stream.md) | The knowledge model MUST keep the player's estimates in a separate number-sense stream for each pair of node and subtype. | approved |
| [REQ-5320](REQ-5320-number-sense-guess-rate.md) | The number-sense stream MUST treat an estimate as a choice of four with a guess rate of 0.25. | approved |
| [REQ-5322](REQ-5322-estimate-stays-out-of-other-estimates.md) | During the MVP, an estimate MUST NOT feed the "on her own" estimate, the fluency estimate or the estimate of node N4 (rounding and estimating a result). | approved |
| [REQ-5324](REQ-5324-label-magnitude.md) | When the exact answer differs from the correct result by a factor of 10 to a whole power other than 0 and the estimate is right, the engine MUST label the attempt `magnitude`. | approved |
| [REQ-5326](REQ-5326-label-magnitude-unaware.md) | When the exact answer differs from the correct result by a factor of 10 to a whole power other than 0 and the estimate is wrong, the engine MUST label the attempt `magnitude_unaware`. | approved |
| [REQ-5328](REQ-5328-label-estimate-off-exact-ok.md) | When the estimate is wrong and the exact answer is right, the engine MUST label the attempt `estimate_off_exact_ok`. | approved |
| [REQ-5330](REQ-5330-estimate-labels-beside-class.md) | The labels `magnitude` and `magnitude_unaware` MUST sit beside the class and trap the engine gives a wrong answer, and never replace a trap or the class «не классифицирована» (unclassified). | approved |
| [REQ-5332](REQ-5332-right-exact-answer-stays-right.md) | An exact answer labelled `estimate_off_exact_ok` MUST count as right in every measure and never as a mistake. | approved |
| [REQ-5334](REQ-5334-estimate-and-check-time-excluded.md) | The time the player spends in the estimate step and in the check field MUST be left out of the answer time that the fluency test and the rapid-guess test read. | approved |
| [REQ-5336](REQ-5336-check-offered-on-bare-expressions.md) | The game MUST offer the inverse check only on a bare expression whose final operation is addition, subtraction, multiplication or division, and never on a word problem, one-step ones included. | approved |
| [REQ-5338](REQ-5338-check-before-submit-only.md) | The game MUST offer the inverse check only before the player submits the first attempt on the task. | approved |
| [REQ-5340](REQ-5340-check-costs-nothing.md) | Using the inverse check MUST cost the player nothing: no guiding threads and no other currency. | approved |
| [REQ-5342](REQ-5342-check-compares-with-task-given.md) | The inverse check MUST compare the player's check result only with the number in the task that her check must reproduce, and never with the correct answer. | approved |
| [REQ-5344](REQ-5344-check-sends-nothing-beyond-expression.md) | Before the player submits the first attempt, the server MUST NOT send the client the correct answer, the result her check should give for her preliminary answer, or any verdict on that preliminary answer. | approved |
| [REQ-5346](REQ-5346-check-shows-only-match.md) | The check field MUST show only whether the player's check result matches, and never the correct answer. | approved |
| [REQ-5348](REQ-5348-check-match-without-verdict-marks.md) | The check field MUST show a match or a mismatch without a tick, a cross or the words «верно» (correct), «неверно» (incorrect) or «ошибка» (mistake). | approved |
| [REQ-5350](REQ-5350-checked-attempt-unassisted.md) | Using the inverse check MUST NOT make an attempt assisted. | approved |
| [REQ-5352](REQ-5352-preliminary-answer-not-an-attempt.md) | The game MUST NOT record the answer the player checks before submitting as an attempt, so the answer she submits after the check is her first attempt. | approved |
| [REQ-5354](REQ-5354-checked-attempt-counts-on-her-own.md) | An unassisted first attempt made after an inverse check MUST feed the "on her own" estimate from the first day, like any other first attempt of its subtype. | approved |
| [REQ-5356](REQ-5356-log-records-check-use.md) | The event log MUST record each use of the inverse check with the preliminary answer, the value the player typed and whether it matched. | approved |
| [REQ-5358](REQ-5358-log-records-estimate.md) | The event log MUST record each estimate with the option the player picked and whether it was right. | approved |
| [REQ-5360](REQ-5360-report-counts-saved-answer.md) | The report MUST count an answer changed after the inverse check as saved when, and only when, it went from wrong to right. | approved |
| [REQ-5362](REQ-5362-report-counts-spoiled-answer.md) | The report MUST count an answer changed after the inverse check as spoiled when it went from right to wrong. | approved |
| [REQ-5364](REQ-5364-report-shows-check-use.md) | The report MUST show the share of the tasks offering the inverse check on which the player used it, per week, beside the numbers of saved and spoiled answers. | approved |
| [REQ-5366](REQ-5366-report-shows-estimate-matrix.md) | The report MUST show, for each node with estimates, a matrix of the estimate right or wrong by the exact answer right or wrong. | approved |
| [REQ-5368](REQ-5368-estimate-matrix-too-little-data.md) | The estimate matrix MUST show «мало данных» (too little data) in each cell that holds fewer than 5 answers. | approved |
| [REQ-5370](REQ-5370-no-correct-estimate-option-before-exact.md) | The server MUST NOT send the client which estimate option is correct, or any value the verdict on the estimate could be worked out from, before the player submits the exact answer of the same task. | approved |
| [REQ-5400](REQ-5400-cant-know-button-every-phase.md) | Every T1 to T4 word problem MUST offer «Нельзя узнать» (can't be known) beside «Не знаю» (I don't know) in every phase it shows, the model choice, the plan cards and the step fields included. | approved |
| [REQ-5402](REQ-5402-cant-know-button-placement.md) | «Нельзя узнать» (can't be known) MUST sit in the task window's action row, with another control or a gap of at least one button's width between it and «Не знаю» (I don't know), and never on the keypad or next to a digit key. | approved |
| [REQ-5404](REQ-5404-cant-know-own-shortcut.md) | On the computer interface, «Нельзя узнать» (can't be known) MUST have a keyboard shortcut of its own, different from the shortcut of «Не знаю» (I don't know) and from every key that enters an answer. | approved |
| [REQ-5406](REQ-5406-unanswerable-looks-solvable.md) | An unanswerable problem MUST show the same phases and fields as a solvable problem of its tier and answer form, the model choice or plan and the step fields included. | approved |
| [REQ-5408](REQ-5408-unanswerable-has-full-content.md) | Every unanswerable problem MUST have the content of a complete problem with one given withheld: a model choice, step fields, hint rungs and plan cards built from that complete problem. | approved |
| [REQ-5410](REQ-5410-unanswerable-step-count.md) | The step count of an unanswerable problem MUST be the step count of the complete problem it withholds a given from. | approved |
| [REQ-5412](REQ-5412-whats-missing-four-options.md) | Every T1 to T4 word problem MUST have 4 options for what is missing, each naming a quantity of the problem's story that its text doesn't state. | approved |
| [REQ-5414](REQ-5414-packet-hides-problem-kind.md) | Before the player answers, the server MUST NOT send anything that lets a client tell an unanswerable problem from a solvable one of the same tier and answer form. | approved |
| [REQ-5416](REQ-5416-insufficient-correct-verdict.md) | When the player answers «Нельзя узнать» (can't be known) on an unanswerable problem and chooses the withheld given, the server MUST record the verdict `insufficient_correct` with credit 1 and the outcome `clean`. | approved |
| [REQ-5418](REQ-5418-insufficient-partial-verdict.md) | When the player answers «Нельзя узнать» (can't be known) on an unanswerable problem and chooses a wrong given or none, the server MUST record the verdict `insufficient_partial` with credit 0.5 and the outcome `partial`. | approved |
| [REQ-5420](REQ-5420-whats-missing-choice-optional.md) | The game MUST accept «Нельзя узнать» (can't be known) as an answer without a choice of what is missing. | approved |
| [REQ-5422](REQ-5422-false-insufficient-verdict.md) | When the player answers «Нельзя узнать» (can't be known) on a solvable word problem, the server MUST record the verdict `false_insufficient` as a wrong answer, with credit 0, the outcome `alt` and the badge `soft`. | approved |
| [REQ-5424](REQ-5424-insufficient-verdict-scores.md) | The knowledge model MUST score `insufficient_correct` as 1, `insufficient_partial` as 0.5 and `false_insufficient` as 0. | approved |
| [REQ-5426](REQ-5426-cant-know-apart-from-dont-know.md) | The event log MUST keep each «Нельзя узнать» (can't be known) answer apart from «Не знаю» (I don't know), with the verdicts `insufficient_correct`, `insufficient_partial` and `false_insufficient` distinct from "don't know" and from each other. | approved |
| [REQ-5428](REQ-5428-number-on-unanswerable-class.md) | When the player gives a number as the answer to an unanswerable problem, the checker MUST give it the error class `answered_insufficient`, as a wrong answer with credit 0. | approved |
| [REQ-5430](REQ-5430-used-extra-data-class.md) | When the player's answer to a word problem equals the result of a solution that uses the problem's unused number, the checker MUST give it the error class `used_extra_data`. | approved |
| [REQ-5432](REQ-5432-surplus-answers-distinct.md) | The generator MUST NOT produce a word problem in which a solution using its unused number gives the correct answer or the answer of another trap. | approved |
| [REQ-5434](REQ-5434-surplus-share-t1-t3.md) | About 10 % of the T1 to T3 word problems the player gets on nodes whose ordinary subtype is «понимает» (understands) or above MUST be of a surplus subtype, `T1.surplus` to `T3.surplus`, carrying one number the answer doesn't need. | approved |
| [REQ-5436](REQ-5436-t4-no-surplus-subtype.md) | The skill graph MUST NOT hold a separate subtype `T4.surplus`, because every ordinary T4 problem already carries one unused number and is the surplus form. | approved |
| [REQ-5438](REQ-5438-unanswerable-share.md) | Except while a refusal halves it, about 5 % of the T1 to T4 word problems the player gets on nodes whose ordinary subtype is «понимает» (understands) or above MUST be unanswerable problems. | approved |
| [REQ-5440](REQ-5440-surplus-unanswerable-random-draw.md) | The game MUST choose which word problems are surplus or unanswerable at random, on no fixed cadence. | approved |
| [REQ-5442](REQ-5442-insufficient-subtype-names.md) | The skill graph MUST name the unanswerable subtypes `T1.insufficient` to `T4.insufficient`. | approved |
| [REQ-5444](REQ-5444-s3-missing-keeps-meaning.md) | The subtype `S3.missing` MUST keep its meaning, finding the missing number from a mean. | approved |
| [REQ-5446](REQ-5446-new-subtypes-sibling-prerequisite.md) | The Director MUST NOT choose a surplus or an unanswerable subtype of a T node until the ordinary subtype of the same node is «понимает» (understands) or above. | approved |
| [REQ-5448](REQ-5448-stretch-subtypes-sibling-prerequisite.md) | The Director MUST NOT choose the stretch subtypes `G6.blocks` and `S3.missing` until the ordinary subtypes of the same node are «Бегло» (fluent) or «Устойчиво» (stable). | approved |
| [REQ-5450](REQ-5450-new-subtypes-weight-after-activation.md) | Once a model version that uses them passes the held-out comparison of RES-0900 conclusion 25 and is activated, each surplus and unanswerable subtype of T1 to T4 MUST carry a fixed weight of 0.1 in its node. | approved |
| [REQ-5452](REQ-5452-new-subtypes-out-of-t-estimates.md) | Observations of the surplus and unanswerable subtypes MUST stay out of the estimates of nodes T1 to T4 and the "on her own" estimate until a model version that uses them passes the held-out comparison of RES-0900 conclusion 25 and is activated. | approved |
| [REQ-5454](REQ-5454-refusal-guard-window.md) | When the player has answered «Нельзя узнать» (can't be known) on 3 or more of her last 20 solvable T1 to T4 word problems, the report MUST show the observation «склонна отказываться от задачи» (tends to refuse the problem). | approved |
| [REQ-5456](REQ-5456-refusal-guard-lowers-share.md) | When the player has answered «Нельзя узнать» (can't be known) on 3 or more of her last 20 solvable T1 to T4 word problems, the Director MUST hold the share of unanswerable problems at half its usual value, halving it once and never again, until a later window of 20 solvable T1 to T4 word problems holds 2 or fewer such answers. | approved |
| [REQ-5458](REQ-5458-cant-know-outside-dont-know-runs.md) | An answer of «Нельзя узнать» (can't be known) MUST NOT count as «Не знаю» (I don't know) in a run of 3 «Не знаю» in a row, which logs an avoidance signal and offers a rest stop. | approved |
| [REQ-5460](REQ-5460-cant-know-outside-help-share.md) | An answer of «Нельзя узнать» (can't be known) MUST NOT count as «Не знаю» (I don't know) in the help limit or in the help share that flags an adventure and raises review. | approved |
| [REQ-5462](REQ-5462-report-separate-refusal-counts.md) | Beside the word-problem matrix, the report MUST show as separate counts «Не знаю» (I don't know), «Нельзя узнать» (can't be known) on solvable problems, answers of class `used_extra_data` and numbers given for unanswerable problems. | approved |
| [REQ-5464](REQ-5464-report-explains-sparse-streams.md) | Beside the streams of the unanswerable subtypes, the report MUST tell the parent that at about one such problem in two to three weeks these estimates stay unchecked or near their prior for months. | approved |
| [REQ-5466](REQ-5466-unanswerable-no-dutch-keywords.md) | An unanswerable problem MUST NOT carry any Dutch keyword of the Dutch bridge. | approved |
| [REQ-5470](REQ-5470-cant-know-own-label.md) | The button that claims a word problem can't be answered MUST carry the label «Нельзя узнать» (can't be known), different from the label «Не знаю» (I don't know). | approved |
| [REQ-5472](REQ-5472-withheld-given-among-options.md) | On an unanswerable problem, one of the 4 options for what is missing MUST name the given the problem withholds. | approved |
| [REQ-5500](REQ-5500-grouping-task-links-then-answer.md) | A grouping task MUST show an expression with a convenient grouping, let the player link two numbers with taps or mark one number to round, and then take her answer. | approved |
| [REQ-5502](REQ-5502-grouping-links-can-be-skipped.md) | A grouping task MUST let the player skip the links and enter her answer straight away, at no cost in guiding threads. | approved |
| [REQ-5504](REQ-5504-grouping-scored-three-values.md) | When the player submits an answer on a grouping task, the engine MUST score the links she submitted with it as `optimal`, `valid` or `none`, apart from the answer. | approved |
| [REQ-5506](REQ-5506-grouping-scored-without-language-model.md) | The engine MUST score a grouping without a language model. | approved |
| [REQ-5508](REQ-5508-grouping-never-an-error.md) | The game and the report MUST NOT present any grouping score as an error. | approved |
| [REQ-5510](REQ-5510-grouping-leaves-outcome-unchanged.md) | The grouping score MUST NOT change a task's outcome. | approved |
| [REQ-5512](REQ-5512-grouping-template-declares-plans.md) | Every grouping template MUST declare its host node, its one technique, its admissible links and its optimal plans. | approved |
| [REQ-5514](REQ-5514-grouping-host-node-holds-calculation.md) | A grouping template's host node MUST be the node of the RES-0800 node table that holds the plain calculation, or A13 when the item's calculation meets A13's prerequisites A7 and A11. | approved |
| [REQ-5516](REQ-5516-grouping-technique-one-of-four.md) | A grouping template's technique MUST be one of four: the commutative and associative laws, rounding with a correction, the distributive law, or convenient pairs such as 25 and 4, 125 and 8, and 50 and 2. | approved |
| [REQ-5518](REQ-5518-rounded-factor-is-rounding.md) | A grouping template for a product with one factor to round, such as `99 * 6`, MUST declare rounding with a correction as its technique. | approved |
| [REQ-5520](REQ-5520-report-omits-distributive-law.md) | The report MUST NOT show a row for the distributive law among the grouping techniques. | approved |
| [REQ-5522](REQ-5522-optimal-means-full-plan.md) | The engine MUST score a grouping `optimal` only when the submitted links equal one full optimal plan of the template, and every other non-empty admissible set of links `valid`. | approved |
| [REQ-5524](REQ-5524-grouping-expressions-all-links-admissible.md) | A grouping template MUST use only expressions in which every link is admissible: all additions, all multiplications, or one number to round. | approved |
| [REQ-5526](REQ-5526-short-loop-only-clean-optimal.md) | The short loop reward, «Короткая петля» (The short loop), MUST give 1 star yarn to a `clean` unassisted first attempt with an `optimal` grouping, and to no other attempt. | approved |
| [REQ-5528](REQ-5528-short-loop-shown-outside-task-window.md) | When the player earns the short loop, the game MUST play the special spell animation and show the System line «Обнаружен короткий путь. Длинный путь обиделся» (A short path was found. The long path took offence) outside the task window. | approved |
| [REQ-5530](REQ-5530-short-loop-chosen-from-content.md) | The engine MUST choose the short loop's animation and System line from content, and never from the Master. | approved |
| [REQ-5532](REQ-5532-short-loop-reads-no-time.md) | The short loop MUST NOT read any time field, so no part of it depends on how fast the player answers. | approved |
| [REQ-5534](REQ-5534-weekly-yarn-measured-in-stage-0-3.md) | During stage 0.3, the game MUST give the owner, for each week, the star yarn the player earned per source, taken from the event log, with the short loop as a source of its own. | approved |
| [REQ-5536](REQ-5536-high-yarn-opens-recipe-record.md) | When the measured star yarn is above 60 in each of two weeks in a row, the owner MUST open a new record that resizes the forge recipes. | approved |
| [REQ-5538](REQ-5538-grouping-outside-knowledge-model.md) | A grouping-task attempt MUST stay out of the "on her own" estimate, the fluency estimate, blocks, probes and node states until a model version that uses it passes the held-out comparison of RES-0900 conclusion 25 and is activated. | approved |
| [REQ-5540](REQ-5540-grouping-outside-model-in-mvp.md) | During the MVP, the game MUST NOT activate a model version that takes grouping-task attempts into any estimate, state, probe or block. | approved |
| [REQ-5542](REQ-5542-rational-calculation-stream.md) | The server MUST keep a separate «рациональный счёт» (rational calculation) stream per technique, holding each grouping score with its host node and whether it was assisted. | approved |
| [REQ-5544](REQ-5544-rational-stream-recomputed-from-log.md) | The rational calculation stream MUST be recomputable from the event log alone, giving the same figures as the stream held before the recompute. | approved |
| [REQ-5546](REQ-5546-one-grouping-task-per-floor.md) | The Director MUST offer at most 1 grouping task a floor. | approved |
| [REQ-5548](REQ-5548-grouping-needs-understands.md) | The Director MUST offer a grouping task only when its host node's tested state is at least «Понимает» (understands). | approved |
| [REQ-5550](REQ-5550-grouping-keeps-graded-minimum.md) | The Director MUST offer a grouping task only when the forecast, without counting that task, still leaves the adventure its minimum of graded first attempts. | approved |
