| [REQ-5552](REQ-5552-grouping-outside-graded-count.md) | A grouping-task attempt MUST NOT count towards an adventure's minimum of graded first attempts. | approved |
| [REQ-5554](REQ-5554-room-slot-four-sources.md) | The Director MUST fill every room slot from one of four sources: the frontier, spaced review, the parent's lesson topics or a grouping task. | approved |
| [REQ-5556](REQ-5556-grouping-never-in-mental-arithmetic.md) | The Director MUST NOT place a grouping task in mental arithmetic. | approved |
| [REQ-5558](REQ-5558-grouping-slot-source-logged.md) | When the Director fills a room slot with a grouping task, the event log MUST record the slot's source as a grouping task, apart from the other three sources. | approved |
| [REQ-5560](REQ-5560-tappable-number-touch-zone.md) | Every tappable number in a grouping task MUST have a touch zone of at least 56 px in both directions. | approved |
| [REQ-5562](REQ-5562-grouping-plans-withheld-from-client.md) | Before a grouping task's first attempt ends, the server MUST NOT send the client its optimal plans or any score of the links. | approved |
| [REQ-5564](REQ-5564-task-window-never-marks-links.md) | The task window MUST NOT mark a link as optimal, valid or wrong. | approved |
| [REQ-5566](REQ-5566-log-records-links-as-drawn.md) | When the player draws or removes a link or a rounding mark on a grouping task, the event log MUST record it. | approved |
| [REQ-5568](REQ-5568-resume-restores-links.md) | When the player resumes a grouping task, the game MUST restore from the event log the links and rounding marks she had drawn before leaving. | approved |
| [REQ-5570](REQ-5570-attempt-records-links-and-score.md) | When the player submits an attempt on a grouping task, the event log MUST record the submitted links and their grouping score. | approved |
| [REQ-5572](REQ-5572-grouping-fact-one-owner.md) | The event log MUST record each submitted grouping in one event only. | approved |
| [REQ-5574](REQ-5574-assisted-grouping-recorded.md) | When the player buys a hint rung on a grouping task or answers its second attempt, the event log MUST record the grouping as assisted. | approved |
| [REQ-5576](REQ-5576-assisted-grouping-outside-unassisted-figures.md) | An assisted grouping MUST stay out of the rational calculation stream's unassisted figures. | approved |
| [REQ-5578](REQ-5578-grouping-short-solution-shows-plan.md) | The short solution of a grouping task MUST show the template's optimal plan beside the direct calculation, whatever grouping the player chose. | approved |
| [REQ-5580](REQ-5580-report-sees-convenient-methods.md) | The report MUST show «видит удобные приёмы» (sees convenient methods) per technique as the share of `optimal` groupings among unassisted first attempts on grouping tasks, with skipped links counted as `none` and the number of attempts beside it. | approved |
| [REQ-5582](REQ-5582-report-assisted-groupings-apart.md) | The report MUST show assisted groupings apart, in the «с помощью» (with help) figures. | approved |
| [REQ-5584](REQ-5584-report-says-figure-is-invited.md) | The report MUST describe the «видит удобные приёмы» figure to the parent as what the player marks when the task invites her to link numbers. | approved |
| [REQ-5586](REQ-5586-report-none-neutral.md) | The report MUST present a `none` grouping as a neutral result and never set it against a norm. | approved |
| [REQ-5588](REQ-5588-a13-hosts-only-its-prerequisites.md) | Node A13 MUST host only grouping items whose calculation meets its prerequisites A7 and A11. | approved |
| [REQ-5590](REQ-5590-recipe-amounts-held-in-stage-0-3.md) | The forge recipe amounts MUST stay as REQ-2172 sets them until a record opened under REQ-5536 resizes them. | approved |
| [REQ-5600](REQ-5600-tier-one-problem-has-no-plan.md) | A word problem of tier T1 MUST NOT open with a plan. | approved |
| [REQ-5602](REQ-5602-task-opens-with-one-modelling-phase.md) | A task MUST NOT open with both a model choice and a plan. | approved |
| [REQ-5604](REQ-5604-guardian-problem-follows-shared-phases.md) | When a Guardian sets a word problem, the problem MUST open with a model choice, a plan or neither, and take step-by-step or final-answer input, by the shares REQ-5606, REQ-5610, REQ-5670 and REQ-0703 set. | approved |
| [REQ-5606](REQ-5606-plan-share-of-compound-problems.md) | Between 20% and 30% of the compound word problems (tiers T2 to T4) the player gets in any 30 days, or within one problem of that range, MUST open with a plan. | approved |
| [REQ-5608](REQ-5608-plan-problems-keep-step-input-band.md) | Between 40% and 60% of the compound word problems that open with a plan in any 30 days, or within one problem of that range, MUST take step-by-step input, and the rest the final answer only. | approved |
| [REQ-5610](REQ-5610-model-choice-share-of-compound-problems.md) | Between 20% and 30% of the compound word problems the player gets in any 30 days, or within one problem of that range, MUST open with a model choice. | approved |
| [REQ-5612](REQ-5612-plan-runs-in-solving-order.md) | A plan MUST ask the player to lay the needed cards in the order of solving, from the first quantity to find to the problem's question as the last card. | approved |
| [REQ-5614](REQ-5614-plan-decoy-count-by-tier.md) | A plan MUST show exactly 1 decoy card on a T2 or T4 problem, and 1 or 2 on a T3 problem. | approved |
| [REQ-5616](REQ-5616-plan-cards-from-one-solution-path.md) | The needed cards of a plan MUST come from exactly one valid solution path of the problem, one card per step, with the problem's question as the last card. | approved |
| [REQ-5618](REQ-5618-decoy-never-a-valid-step.md) | A decoy card MUST NOT ask for a quantity that a step of any valid solution path of the problem finds. | approved |
| [REQ-5620](REQ-5620-plan-has-one-correct-card-set.md) | Among the cards a plan shows, exactly one set of cards MUST make a correct plan. | approved |
| [REQ-5622](REQ-5622-decoy-is-one-of-two-kinds.md) | Each decoy card MUST ask either for a quantity the problem's text already states or for a quantity that only a structure trap or the surplus datum computes. | approved |
| [REQ-5624](REQ-5624-stated-quantity-decoy-scores-extra-step.md) | When the player lays a decoy that asks for a quantity the text states, and no label of higher precedence applies, the engine MUST score the plan `extra_step`. | approved |
| [REQ-5626](REQ-5626-trap-decoy-scores-used-distractor.md) | When the player lays a decoy that asks for a quantity only a structure trap or the surplus datum computes, the engine MUST score the plan `used_distractor`. | approved |
| [REQ-5628](REQ-5628-plan-missing-card-scores-missing-step.md) | When the player leaves out a needed card and lays no trap or surplus decoy, the engine MUST score the plan `missing_step`. | approved |
| [REQ-5630](REQ-5630-plan-order-follows-dependencies.md) | The engine MUST score a plan `wrong_order` when, and only when, a card stands before a card that finds a quantity the first card needs, and no label of higher precedence applies. | approved |
| [REQ-5632](REQ-5632-plan-correct-when-no-fault.md) | The engine MUST score a plan `correct` when it holds every needed card and no decoy, and every card stands after each card that finds a quantity it needs. | approved |
| [REQ-5634](REQ-5634-plan-label-precedence.md) | When a plan has several faults, the engine MUST give it the first label that applies in the order `used_distractor`, `missing_step`, `extra_step`, `wrong_order`. | approved |
| [REQ-5636](REQ-5636-attempt-logs-plan-choice.md) | When a problem opened with a plan, the event log MUST record with the attempt its `planChoice` as one of `correct`, `extra_step`, `missing_step`, `wrong_order` and `used_distractor`, apart from the answer. | approved |
| [REQ-5638](REQ-5638-log-holds-plan-card-sequence.md) | The event log MUST record the full sequence of cards the player laid in each plan. | approved |
| [REQ-5640](REQ-5640-plan-submitted-event.md) | When the player submits a plan, the event log MUST record a `plan_submitted` event holding the full sequence of cards she laid, before she answers the problem. | approved |
| [REQ-5642](REQ-5642-resume-restores-submitted-plan.md) | When the player resumes a problem whose plan she submitted but hadn't yet answered, the game MUST restore her plan and her place in the solving phase. | approved |
| [REQ-5644](REQ-5644-plan-label-feeds-no-measure.md) | A plan's label MUST NOT change the answer's credit, a room or floor outcome, the success share, the holding-steps value or any skill estimate. | approved |
| [REQ-5646](REQ-5646-answer-after-plan-counts.md) | The answer to a problem that opened with a plan MUST count towards credit, outcomes and measures as the answer to a problem that opened with no phase does. | approved |
| [REQ-5648](REQ-5648-attempt-logs-opening-phase.md) | The event log MUST record, with every attempt on a word problem, which phase the problem opened with: none, a model choice or a plan. | approved |
| [REQ-5650](REQ-5650-task-window-shows-no-plan-result.md) | The task window MUST NOT show any result of the plan before the player answers the problem. | approved |
| [REQ-5652](REQ-5652-knot-scheme-shows-both-plans.md) | After the player answers a problem that opened with a plan, the knot's scheme MUST show her plan beside the engine's plan. | approved |
| [REQ-5654](REQ-5654-plan-cards-label-step-rows.md) | When a problem opens with a plan and takes step-by-step input, the step rows MUST carry the player's plan cards as their labels, in the order she laid them. | approved |
| [REQ-5656](REQ-5656-step-rows-editable-after-plan.md) | When the step rows carry the player's plan cards, she MUST be able to add and remove step rows. | approved |
| [REQ-5658](REQ-5658-hint-in-plan-assists-plan-and-answer.md) | When the player buys a hint rung during the plan phase, the event log MUST record both the plan and the answer as assisted. | approved |
| [REQ-5660](REQ-5660-plan-cards-without-language-model.md) | A language model MUST NOT write or choose the text of a plan card. | approved |
| [REQ-5662](REQ-5662-plan-card-text-per-language.md) | Each plan card's wording MUST be kept in the per-language player strings, one wording per step of each valid solution path and per decoy quantity the problem's template can offer, with the numbers filled in when the problem is built. | approved |
| [REQ-5664](REQ-5664-report-shows-planning-errors-apart.md) | The report MUST show a planning error in a word problem, a plan scored anything other than `correct`, apart from a modelling error and a calculation error. | approved |
| [REQ-5666](REQ-5666-report-crosses-plan-and-model-with-answer.md) | The report MUST show word problem results as two crosses sharing the answer axis: model choice by answer on problems that opened with a model choice, and plan by answer on problems that opened with a plan. | approved |
| [REQ-5668](REQ-5668-wrong-answer-after-right-plan-is-calculation.md) | The report MUST count a wrong answer after a `correct` plan as a calculation error. | approved |
| [REQ-5670](REQ-5670-model-choice-share-of-tier-one-problems.md) | Between 20% and 30% of the T1 word problems the player gets in any 30 days, or within one problem of that range, MUST open with a model choice. | approved |
| [REQ-5672](REQ-5672-plan-graded-by-labels-not-permutation.md) | The engine MUST grade a plan only by its five labels, never by the exact-permutation rule REQ-0772 sets for order answers. | approved |
| [REQ-5700](REQ-5700-puzzle-branch-optional-in-diary.md) | The game MUST offer the maths puzzles of «Петельки Смотрителя» (the Keeper's loops) on pages of the Keeper's Diary as play the player enters only by her own choice. | approved |
| [REQ-5702](REQ-5702-one-new-puzzle-a-day.md) | The game MUST offer the player at most one new puzzle in a game day. | approved |
| [REQ-5704](REQ-5704-three-puzzles-open-at-most.md) | The game MUST keep at most three puzzles open at once, where a puzzle in the box doesn't count as open and a puzzle taken back out of the box does, so with three open she puts one away before she takes one out. | approved |
| [REQ-5706](REQ-5706-puzzle-outside-adventure-plan.md) | A puzzle MUST take no slot in the adventure and no place in the flow limit, the domain coverage rule or the day's plan. | approved |
| [REQ-5708](REQ-5708-unsolved-puzzle-costs-nothing.md) | When the player leaves a puzzle unsolved or puts it away, the game MUST NOT take away or withhold any experience, star yarn, star-steel shards, buttons, guiding threads, quest progress, streak or title she has or would otherwise get. | approved |
| [REQ-5710](REQ-5710-branch-and-puzzle-names.md) | Wherever a Russian text the player or the parent sees names the branch or one puzzle, it MUST call them «Петельки Смотрителя» (the Keeper's loops) and «головоломка» (puzzle). | approved |
| [REQ-5712](REQ-5712-puzzle-names-hide-mystery.md) | The name of the branch or of any puzzle MUST NOT contain «узелок», Irma's name or any word for the Keeper's familiar. | approved |
| [REQ-5714](REQ-5714-puzzle-page-signed-k.md) | Every puzzle page MUST be signed «С.» (K., for «Смотрительница», Keeper), except a page that carries «И.» as a running clue under REQ-5716. | approved |
| [REQ-5716](REQ-5716-puzzle-clues-keep-canon-pace.md) | A puzzle MUST carry the signature «И.» or another running clue of the campaign year only as one of those clues, counted in their pace of no more than one clue every several sessions. | approved |
| [REQ-5718](REQ-5718-new-puzzle-only-after-finale.md) | The game MUST offer a new puzzle only after the day's finale. | approved |
| [REQ-5720](REQ-5720-first-puzzle-from-second-adventure.md) | The game MUST offer the first puzzle no earlier than the finale of the player's second adventure. | approved |
| [REQ-5722](REQ-5722-open-puzzle-at-rest-stop.md) | When the player is at a rest stop and has an open puzzle, the game MUST let her open it and work on it there. | approved |
| [REQ-5724](REQ-5724-puzzle-leaves-campfire-scene.md) | When the player opens a puzzle at a rest stop, the game MUST end the campfire scene. | approved |
| [REQ-5725](REQ-5725-closed-puzzle-returns-to-adventure.md) | When the player closes a puzzle she opened at a rest stop, the game MUST return her to the adventure's next step after the rest stop. | approved |
| [REQ-5726](REQ-5726-eye-counter-excludes-campfire-only.md) | The 20-minute count towards the next eye exercise MUST exclude the time of eye exercises and of the rest stop's campfire scene, and no other active time. | approved |
| [REQ-5728](REQ-5728-puzzle-time-is-active-time.md) | Time the player spends on a puzzle MUST count in the day's active time that brings the soft stop. | approved |
| [REQ-5730](REQ-5730-soft-stop-on-puzzle-time.md) | When, after the day's finale, the day's active time reaches the soft-stop point while the player is on a puzzle, the game MUST bring the soft stop at the next boundary. | approved |
| [REQ-5731](REQ-5731-puzzle-soft-stop-offers-put-away.md) | When the soft stop comes on puzzle time after the day's finale, it MUST offer to put the puzzle away beside «Ещё один ряд» (One more row). | approved |
| [REQ-5732](REQ-5732-knowledge-model-ignores-puzzles.md) | The knowledge model MUST NOT read any puzzle event. | approved |
| [REQ-5734](REQ-5734-puzzle-actions-own-event-types.md) | The event log MUST record every step of the player's play with a puzzle under a puzzle event type of its own, `puzzle_offered`, `puzzle_opened`, `puzzle_move`, `puzzle_attempt`, `puzzle_hint`, `puzzle_solved` or `puzzle_shelved`, and never as a graded attempt. | approved |
| [REQ-5736](REQ-5736-no-new-topic-lessons-but-puzzles.md) | Apart from a puzzle's solution page in the Diary, the game MUST NOT offer lessons that teach a new maths topic. | approved |
| [REQ-5738](REQ-5738-puzzle-numbers-from-approved-data.md) | Every number in a puzzle's statement, hint rungs and solution MUST come from the puzzle's data, which a person wrote or approved, and never from a model at play time; a number a model drafted counts as data only once a person approves it. | approved |
| [REQ-5740](REQ-5740-puzzle-check-accepts-any-valid-answer.md) | A puzzle's check MUST accept every answer that satisfies the puzzle's rules, and not only a stored answer. | approved |
| [REQ-5742](REQ-5742-reference-solution-passes-before-review.md) | A puzzle MUST enter the parent's review queue only after its stored reference solution passes the puzzle's check. | approved |
| [REQ-5744](REQ-5744-puzzle-blind-solve-statement-only.md) | The blind solve of a puzzle MUST show the checking model the statement only, with no answer and no candidate answers. | approved |
| [REQ-5746](REQ-5746-puzzle-blind-solve-passes-on-check.md) | A puzzle MUST enter the parent's review queue only after its check accepts the checking model's free-form answer from the blind solve. | approved |
| [REQ-5748](REQ-5748-puzzle-preparation-offline-roles.md) | The offline preparation of the puzzle bank MUST call models only in offline roles on the offline key. | approved |
| [REQ-5750](REQ-5750-puzzle-run-own-budget.md) | An offline puzzle run MUST stop at a puzzle budget of its own, apart from every other budget. | approved |
| [REQ-5752](REQ-5752-serve-only-approved-puzzles.md) | The game MUST serve only puzzles whose current content the parent approved. | approved |
| [REQ-5754](REQ-5754-puzzle-approval-recorded-with-hash.md) | The event log MUST record each puzzle approval with a hash of the exact content the parent approved. | approved |
| [REQ-5756](REQ-5756-puzzle-review-shows-whole-puzzle.md) | For each puzzle, the review screen MUST show the statement, the three hint rungs, the full solution, the check's result on the reference solution and the source of the idea. | approved |
| [REQ-5758](REQ-5758-hint-rungs-without-model-in-play.md) | Every hint rung MUST reach the player with no call to a language model during play, either built from the task's template or taken from the approved puzzle bank. | approved |
| [REQ-5760](REQ-5760-puzzle-rungs-approved-fixed-text.md) | A puzzle's three hint rungs MUST be fixed text the parent approved with the puzzle. | approved |
| [REQ-5762](REQ-5762-puzzle-strings-pass-forbidden-list.md) | Every puzzle string, the familiar's lines after a wrong answer included, MUST pass the forbidden list in every word form. | approved |
| [REQ-5764](REQ-5764-puzzle-data-language-free.md) | A puzzle's numbers, rules and check MUST NOT depend on the display language. | approved |
| [REQ-5766](REQ-5766-rebus-in-digits-or-shapes.md) | A rebus MUST be written in digits or shapes, and never in the letters of a language. | approved |
| [REQ-5768](REQ-5768-puzzle-names-idea-source.md) | Each puzzle in the bank MUST name the source of its idea. | approved |
| [REQ-5770](REQ-5770-puzzle-text-written-anew.md) | A puzzle's statement, story, figure and solution MUST be written anew, and never copied or scanned from the source of its idea. | approved |
| [REQ-5772](REQ-5772-solved-puzzle-page-and-mark.md) | A solved puzzle MUST give a Diary page with its solution in the Keeper's hand and a mark in the collection for its theme. | approved |
| [REQ-5774](REQ-5774-solved-puzzle-star-yarn.md) | A solved puzzle MUST give 2 star yarn, and a solved puzzle of the week 3. | approved |
| [REQ-5776](REQ-5776-theme-series-gives-title.md) | Each series of 5 solved puzzles in one theme MUST give a new cosmetic title. | approved |
| [REQ-5778](REQ-5778-puzzle-reward-same-after-hint.md) | A solved puzzle MUST give the same reward whether or not the player took a hint. | approved |
| [REQ-5780](REQ-5780-puzzle-reward-no-adventure-advantage.md) | A puzzle MUST reward the player only with its Diary solution page, its mark in the collection, star yarn, a title and the free guiding thread of REQ-5786. | approved |
| [REQ-5782](REQ-5782-balance-simulation-counts-puzzles.md) | The economy's balance simulation MUST include the star yarn that puzzles give and the guiding threads they spend. | approved |
| [REQ-5784](REQ-5784-puzzle-answers-unlimited.md) | The game MUST let the player answer a puzzle as many times as she likes. | approved |
| [REQ-5786](REQ-5786-free-thread-after-two-wrong.md) | When the player gives a puzzle its second wrong answer, the familiar MUST offer her one free guiding thread for that puzzle's hint ladder, once for each puzzle. | approved |
| [REQ-5788](REQ-5788-no-third-attempt-on-adventure-task.md) | The game MUST NOT give a third attempt on an adventure task. | approved |
| [REQ-5790](REQ-5790-week-puzzle-within-limits.md) | The puzzle of the week MUST count as that day's one new puzzle and as one of the three open puzzles. | approved |
| [REQ-5792](REQ-5792-verify-checks-each-puzzle.md) | The build's verification MUST fail on any puzzle that breaks REQ-5742, REQ-5762, REQ-5764, REQ-5766 or REQ-5768, and on no count of puzzles in a theme. | approved |
| [REQ-5794](REQ-5794-puzzle-theme-rotation.md) | Unless no theme with an approved puzzle left meets this rule, the Director MUST NOT offer a theme again until at least 4 other themes have come since it last came. | approved |
| [REQ-5795](REQ-5795-puzzle-theme-needs-approved-puzzle.md) | The Director MUST choose a new puzzle's theme only among the themes that have an approved puzzle left to offer. | approved |
| [REQ-5796](REQ-5796-report-shows-non-standard-thinking.md) | Report v1 MUST show the section «Нестандартное мышление» (non-standard thinking) on the VWO readiness screen beside the ceiling, labelled as a reserve and not an assessment. | approved |
| [REQ-5798](REQ-5798-non-standard-thinking-contents.md) | The section «Нестандартное мышление» (non-standard thinking) MUST show the puzzles solved in each theme, the mean hint rung, the puzzles the player came back to by herself, the puzzles solved without a hint on the second or third game day after they were offered, and the puzzles offered, opened and shelved. | approved |
| [REQ-5800](REQ-5800-cito-statement-entry-fields.md) | Each statement about Cito that the game's logic or report text depends on MUST be stored as its own entry holding the statement and, unless the entry is marked "unconfirmed", the URL of the Cito page it rests on, a verbatim quotation from that page and the date the quotation was checked. | approved |
| [REQ-5802](REQ-5802-cito-entry-unconfirmed-mark.md) | When a Cito entry has no URL, or its quotation no longer appears on its page, the entry MUST carry the mark "unconfirmed". | approved |
| [REQ-5804](REQ-5804-report-shows-unconfirmed-cito-mark.md) | When a report text rests on a Cito entry marked "unconfirmed", the report MUST show that mark beside the text. | approved |
| [REQ-5806](REQ-5806-cito-check-only-reports.md) | The Cito check, whether the parent starts it in the Parent Room or a person starts it from the command line, MUST do no more than re-read each Cito page, report the pages that changed and the quotations that no longer appear, and set the "unconfirmed" mark on the entries they affect. | approved |
| [REQ-5808](REQ-5808-first-cito-entries.md) | The first Cito entries MUST be the ten claims RES-4080 confirms, each with its quotation and the check date 2026-09-28, and the two claims it leaves unconfirmed, the parent's access to the bare-versus-context split and Cito's multiplication sign, marked "unconfirmed" until a source confirms them. | approved |
| [REQ-5810](REQ-5810-no-copy-of-cito-test.md) | The game MUST NOT show the player a copy of a Cito item, a task laid out as a Cito item or a mock Cito test. | approved |
| [REQ-5812](REQ-5812-no-test-date-or-word-for-player.md) | The player's screens MUST NOT show a Cito test's date, the name Cito or a word naming a test, such as «тест» (test) or «контрольная» (test paper). | approved |
| [REQ-5814](REQ-5814-parent-sets-cito-horizons.md) | The Parent Room MUST let the parent set a date for each Cito horizon, with 2027-01-15 for M7 and 2027-05-15 for E7 until the parent changes it. | approved |
| [REQ-5816](REQ-5816-director-moves-to-next-horizon.md) | When the parent enters a Cito result for a horizon, the Director MUST work toward the next horizon, and with no later horizon left, give block priority no weight until the parent sets a new one. | approved |
| [REQ-5818](REQ-5818-cito-moment-name-unambiguous.md) | Every record and screen that names a Cito test moment MUST use a name that can't be read as a graph node or a science topic, such as `cito:M7`. | approved |
| [REQ-5820](REQ-5820-node-carries-cito-block.md) | Each node and subtype of the skill graph MUST carry a Cito block from 1 to 6 or no block, with no block on every S node and every stretch node. | approved |
| [REQ-5822](REQ-5822-value-includes-block-priority.md) | The Director's value of a node MUST include 1.5 times the node's block priority, which is (7 - block) / 6 while the node's Cito block isn't ready, and 0 once it is ready or when the node carries no block. | approved |
| [REQ-5824](REQ-5824-value-includes-confirmed-school-goal.md) | The Director's value of a node MUST include 1.0 when a school goal reaches the node through a link the parent confirmed, and nothing for a link the parent hasn't confirmed. | approved |
| [REQ-5826](REQ-5826-school-goal-and-lesson-mark-dont-stack.md) | When a confirmed school goal and a fresh lesson mark reach the same node, the node's value MUST include the larger of the school-goal term and the lesson-mark term, never both. | approved |
| [REQ-5828](REQ-5828-cito-terms-keep-director-rules.md) | Over a 60-day simulation with the block-priority and school-goal terms in force, the Director MUST keep the flow corridor, the three-day window, the stretch cap of 2 tasks a day and the rule that no value term rises as the expected chance of success falls. | approved |
| [REQ-5830](REQ-5830-cito-block-ready-rule.md) | A Cito block MUST count as ready when at least 80 % of its nodes and subtypes are fluent or stable and, for blocks 1 and 2, at least 90 % of their facts are also automatic. | approved |
| [REQ-5832](REQ-5832-track-each-basic-fact.md) | The knowledge model MUST give each basic fact, about 300 of them, one of three states over its last 3 shows: «автоматизм» (automatic) as REQ-5834 defines it, «вычисляет» (computes) when the fact isn't automatic but was right on at least 2 of those shows, and «не знает» (doesn't know) otherwise, a fact shown fewer than 2 times included. | approved |
| [REQ-5834](REQ-5834-fact-automatic-rule.md) | A fact MUST count as automatic when it was right and no slower than the fact threshold on 2 of its last 3 shows, the last of them within the past 14 days. | approved |
| [REQ-5836](REQ-5836-fact-threshold-default.md) | The fact threshold MUST default to 3 s from the fact's show to its submission, plus the device's motor correction. | approved |
| [REQ-5838](REQ-5838-fact-threshold-change-versioned.md) | Every change to the fact threshold, the parent's included, MUST create a new threshold version that the event log records. | approved |
| [REQ-5840](REQ-5840-minimum-time-all-basic-facts.md) | The minimum time of every basic fact, multiplying and dividing by 10, 100 and 1000 included, and of every control fact MUST be the motor correction plus 600 ms. | approved |
| [REQ-5842](REQ-5842-repeat-window-exempts-basic-facts.md) | The game MUST NOT show a generated task again, meaning the same template with the same parameters, within 30 days or within the next shows of its subtype numbering 20 % of the subtype's parameter space, whichever ends first, except for basic facts, multiplying and dividing by 10, 100 and 1000 included, and control facts. | approved |
| [REQ-5844](REQ-5844-volley-shape.md) | The «Залп» (Volley) MUST show 8 to 10 basic facts in one window, answered on one keypad without leaving the window. | approved |
| [REQ-5846](REQ-5846-volley-fact-mix.md) | A Volley MUST hold 3 facts that aren't yet automatic, or every such fact when fewer than 3 exist, and fill its other places with automatic facts. | approved |
| [REQ-5848](REQ-5848-volley-reward-by-accuracy.md) | A Volley that ends with at most one wrong answer MUST give 1 star yarn, whatever its answer times. | approved |
| [REQ-5850](REQ-5850-volley-rapid-guess-not-counted.md) | A Volley answer faster than its minimum time MUST count toward neither the Volley's on-target result nor the day's record. | approved |
| [REQ-5852](REQ-5852-volley-record-never-falls.md) | The Volley's record, the highest count of facts on target in any one day across all days, MUST NOT fall after a worse day or a wrong answer. | approved |
| [REQ-5854](REQ-5854-volley-reply-about-fact.md) | The Volley's reply to a wrong answer MUST speak about the fact, never about the player. | approved |
| [REQ-5856](REQ-5856-floor-order-allows-volley.md) | Each maths floor MUST run in this order: an entry scene, an unscored warm-up, either 2 mental arithmetic tasks or one Volley, the Sources track's tasks on a floor that carries them, 1 or 2 rooms of trials, sometimes a Guardian, then the floor chest. | approved |
| [REQ-5858](REQ-5858-volley-share-of-floors.md) | When blocks 1 and 2 hold a fact that isn't automatic, a Volley MUST take the place of the mental arithmetic tasks on 2 floors in 3 before the M7 horizon, and on fewer floors after it. | approved |
| [REQ-5860](REQ-5860-fact-review-schedule.md) | A fact MUST return the day after a wrong answer or one slower than the fact threshold, and after an answer both right and within the threshold, at a longer interval than the one before. | approved |
| [REQ-5862](REQ-5862-template-declares-format.md) | Every template MUST declare its format as bare, an expression with no story in the task window, or context. | approved |
| [REQ-5864](REQ-5864-half-of-scored-tasks-bare.md) | In a node with templates of both formats, at least half of the node's scored tasks in any 30 days MUST be bare. | approved |
| [REQ-5866](REQ-5866-report-by-format.md) | The report MUST show accuracy and fluency for bare tasks and context tasks apart. | approved |
| [REQ-5868](REQ-5868-report-careless-error-share.md) | The report MUST show, for each week and each error class, the share of first attempts that are wrong on nodes that are fluent or stable and on facts that are automatic. | approved |
| [REQ-5870](REQ-5870-careless-errors-observation.md) | When the share of wrong first attempts on fluent, stable or automatic material, over all error classes together, is above 10 % in a week, the report MUST raise the observation «небрежные ошибки на знакомом» (careless errors on familiar material). | approved |
| [REQ-5872](REQ-5872-open-1s-nodes-ahead-of-school.md) | A node at level 1S up to the end of group 8 MUST open for tasks as soon as its prerequisites are ready, whatever the school has reached. | approved |
| [REQ-5874](REQ-5874-report-mastered-beyond-school.md) | The report MUST list the nodes in the state fluent or stable whose typical school group is later than the player's current group as the parent set it. | approved |
| [REQ-5876](REQ-5876-home-scale-changes-no-estimate.md) | The home skill scale MUST NOT change any estimate or state of the knowledge model. | approved |
| [REQ-5878](REQ-5878-home-scale-not-a-cito-score.md) | The report MUST label the home skill scale as not a Cito score. | approved |
| [REQ-5880](REQ-5880-home-scale-refit-growing-pupils.md) | On simulated pupils whose ability grows during the simulation, the refit of the home scale's item difficulties MUST recover the true item difficulties with a root mean square error of at most 0.3 logits and each pupil's true ability gain within 25 %. | approved |
| [REQ-5882](REQ-5882-cito-result-form-fields.md) | The Parent Room MUST hold a form for a Cito result with these fields: the test moment, the test taken and its level, the vaardigheidsscore, the functioneringsniveau with "<" and ">" allowed, the referentieniveau, the level on the I to V or the A to E scale, the subject, an optional split between bare and context items, an optional expected test advice and a note. | approved |
| [REQ-5884](REQ-5884-cito-result-form-accepts-empty.md) | The Cito result form MUST save a result with any field left empty except the test moment and the subject. | approved |
| [REQ-5886](REQ-5886-dutch-memo-for-school.md) | The Parent Room MUST carry a memo in Dutch listing what the parent can ask the school for, the level of the test taken and the bare-versus-context split included. | approved |
| [REQ-5888](REQ-5888-fact-heat-maps.md) | The report MUST show a 10 x 10 heat map of the multiplication facts and a second one of the division facts, each cell showing its fact's state. | approved |
| [REQ-5890](REQ-5890-report-block-readiness.md) | The report MUST show, each week and for each Cito block, the share of its nodes and subtypes that are fluent or stable and, for blocks 1 and 2, the share of their facts that are automatic, beside whether the block counts as ready for the nearest horizon. | approved |
| [REQ-5892](REQ-5892-printable-not-automatic-list.md) | The report MUST offer a printable list of the facts that aren't yet automatic, ordering no topics and setting no dates. | approved |
| [REQ-5894](REQ-5894-bridge-only-where-understood.md) | The game MUST show a Dutch bridge word only in a task of a node the player already solves at «понимает» (understands) or better without Dutch. | approved |
| [REQ-5896](REQ-5896-bridge-off-removes-all.md) | When the parent turns the Dutch bridge off, the player MUST see no bridge word, bridge card or mixed task anywhere in the game. | approved |
| [REQ-5898](REQ-5898-multiplication-sign-parent-setting.md) | Task text rendered in Russian MUST write multiplication with the sign the parent set, «×» by default or «·», and division with «:». | approved |
| [REQ-5900](REQ-5900-sources-track-beside-graph.md) | The skill graph MUST hold the five Sources track nodes, I1 to I5, as a track of their own, outside the 79 maths nodes and outside the nine domains. | approved |
| [REQ-5902](REQ-5902-track-nodes-carry-no-cito-block.md) | Every Sources track node MUST name no Cito block. | approved |
| [REQ-5904](REQ-5904-model-keeps-track-node-states.md) | The knowledge model MUST keep an estimate and a state for each Sources track node. | approved |
| [REQ-5906](REQ-5906-track-state-from-track-first-attempts.md) | A Sources track node's estimate and state MUST follow from unassisted first attempts on track tasks alone. | approved |
| [REQ-5908](REQ-5908-track-attempt-leaves-graph-alone.md) | An attempt on a Sources track task MUST NOT update the estimate of any graph node. | approved |
| [REQ-5910](REQ-5910-track-outside-on-her-own-in-mvp.md) | During the MVP, an attempt on a Sources track task MUST NOT enter the «сама» (on her own) estimate of the graph. | approved |
| [REQ-5912](REQ-5912-two-track-tasks-per-host-day.md) | When the Director plans an adventure day that holds at least one host floor, it MUST plan 2 Sources track tasks on the first host floor of that plan. | approved |
| [REQ-5914](REQ-5914-track-task-every-three-adventure-days.md) | The Director MUST plan host floors so that in any 3 consecutive adventure days the player completes at least one floor carrying a Sources track task. | approved |
| [REQ-5916](REQ-5916-simulation-counts-track-apart.md) | The day-plan simulation MUST report its first attempts on Sources track tasks as a count of its own, apart from the graph first attempts. | approved |
| [REQ-5918](REQ-5918-track-host-floors.md) | A Sources track task MUST appear only on a floor of the S, M, G or P domain. | approved |
| [REQ-5920](REQ-5920-i5-after-two-understands.md) | The Director MUST NOT offer a task on I5 until at least two of I1 to I4 are in the state «понимает» (understands) or above. | approved |
| [REQ-5922](REQ-5922-track-task-takes-no-room-slot.md) | A Sources track task MUST come as a fixed part of its host floor, like the mental arithmetic tasks, and never take a room slot. | approved |
| [REQ-5924](REQ-5924-no-claim-school-gives-test.md) | The report and the Parent Room MUST NOT claim that the player's school gives a Studievaardigheden test. | approved |
| [REQ-5926](REQ-5926-track-attempts-outside-attempt-minimum.md) | A first attempt on a Sources track task MUST NOT count toward the minimum of 28 scored first attempts in an adventure. | approved |
| [REQ-5928](REQ-5928-simulation-reaches-minimum-with-track.md) | A simulated adventure of 60 minutes that includes its Sources track tasks MUST yield at least 28 scored first attempts on graph tasks when answer times equal 1.0 times the fluency threshold. | approved |
| [REQ-5930](REQ-5930-track-tasks-contextual.md) | Every Sources track task MUST be set in a story context, never as a bare exercise. | approved |
| [REQ-5932](REQ-5932-source-rebuilt-from-seed.md) | The same template, template version and seed MUST rebuild exactly the same source, with every value it holds, for a Sources track task. | approved |
| [REQ-5934](REQ-5934-source-drawn-from-parameters.md) | Every source in a Sources track task, whether a table, chart, timetable or map, MUST be drawn by code from the task's own parameters. | approved |
| [REQ-5936](REQ-5936-source-labels-language-free.md) | A Sources track task's parameters MUST hold no text in any language, so every label its source shows comes from the per-language string file. | approved |
| [REQ-5938](REQ-5938-source-holds-unused-data.md) | Every generated source MUST hold data the task's solution doesn't use. | approved |
| [REQ-5940](REQ-5940-unused-value-beside-read-value.md) | Every generated source MUST place a different value the solution doesn't use next to each value the solution reads: in the adjacent cell of a table or timetable, the adjacent bar or point of a chart, or the adjacent square of a map. | approved |
| [REQ-5942](REQ-5942-source-task-one-correct-answer.md) | For each Sources track template on 10,000 seeds, every generated task MUST have exactly one correct answer or one correct region. | approved |
| [REQ-5944](REQ-5944-question-text-holds-no-answer.md) | The question text of a Sources track task MUST NOT contain the correct answer or any intermediate value of its solution. | approved |
| [REQ-5946](REQ-5946-answer-needs-the-source.md) | The template's solver MUST NOT reach the correct answer of a Sources track task with the source removed. | approved |
| [REQ-5948](REQ-5948-source-task-choice-or-tap.md) | Every task MUST take its answer as free input, except tasks on recognising shapes, nets, symmetry, «объясни, почему» (explain why), natural science and problem models, which take a choice of options, and Sources track tasks, which may also take a choice of at least 4 options or one tap on a region of the source. | approved |
| [REQ-5950](REQ-5950-region-tap-answer.md) | When a Sources track task asks for a region, the player MUST answer with one tap on a cell, a bar or a map square of the source. | approved |
| [REQ-5952](REQ-5952-region-tap-all-or-nothing.md) | A region answer MUST earn credit 1 for the correct region and 0 for any other region. | approved |
| [REQ-5954](REQ-5954-duration-answered-with-unit.md) | When a Sources track task asks for a duration, the player MUST answer it as a number in the field beside its unit label. | approved |
| [REQ-5956](REQ-5956-seven-reading-traps.md) | The Sources track's templates MUST name each reading mistake they detect by one of seven traps: `misread_cell`, `axis_scale`, `legend_misread`, `scale_conversion`, `time_across_hour`, `wrong_source_part` and `ignored_condition`. | approved |
| [REQ-5958](REQ-5958-reading-traps-share-catalogue-rows.md) | The misconceptions screen MUST show `misread_cell` as one row with the S1 neighbouring-cell trap, and `time_across_hour` as one row with the M2 "an hour = 100 minutes" trap. | approved |
| [REQ-5960](REQ-5960-conceptual-reading-traps.md) | The error-type limit MUST class `axis_scale`, `scale_conversion` and `time_across_hour` as conceptual. | approved |
| [REQ-5962](REQ-5962-procedural-reading-traps.md) | The error-type limit MUST class `misread_cell`, `legend_misread`, `wrong_source_part` and `ignored_condition` as procedural. | approved |
| [REQ-5964](REQ-5964-sources-report-screen.md) | Report v1 MUST have a screen «Работа с источниками» (working with sources), apart from the graph screens. | approved |
| [REQ-5966](REQ-5966-sources-screen-contents.md) | The screen «Работа с источниками» (working with sources) MUST show I1 to I5 with their states, first-attempt accuracy by question level, and the reading traps that fired. | approved |
| [REQ-5968](REQ-5968-sources-screen-reading-gaps.md) | The screen «Работа с источниками» (working with sources) MUST show «находит, но не считает» (finds but doesn't calculate) when at least 3 wrong first attempts on track tasks in the last 30 days matched no reading trap, and «считает, но ошибается в чтении» (calculates but misreads) when at least 3 in the same window matched a reading trap. | approved |
| [REQ-5970](REQ-5970-track-outside-vwo-block.md) | The VWO readiness block MUST NOT count any Sources track node in its coverage, margin, ceiling or ladder. | approved |
| [REQ-5972](REQ-5972-track-outside-gap-list.md) | The report's gap list MUST NOT list a Sources track node. | approved |
| [REQ-5974](REQ-5974-source-touch-zone.md) | On the tablet, every tappable part of a source MUST have a touch zone of at least 56 CSS pixels in both directions. | approved |
| [REQ-5976](REQ-5976-source-text-task-size.md) | Text inside a source MUST have a font size of at least 24 CSS pixels, the `task` style, in the normal text size, and at least 28 CSS pixels with large text. | approved |
| [REQ-5978](REQ-5978-text-size-check-reads-sources.md) | The automated text-size check MUST measure text drawn inside sources as well as page text. | approved |
| [REQ-5980](REQ-5980-source-pinch-zoom.md) | The player MUST be able to zoom a source with a pinch inside the source itself. | approved |
| [REQ-5982](REQ-5982-source-zoom-follows-fingers.md) | A pinch zoom on a source MUST follow the player's fingers with no eased transition. | approved |
| [REQ-5984](REQ-5984-source-zoom-leaves-window-still.md) | A pinch zoom on a source MUST leave every other part of the task window where it was. | approved |
| [REQ-5986](REQ-5986-acceptance-test-15-browsers.md) | Acceptance test 15, which snapshots a Sources track task with its drawn source, MUST take its snapshots in WebKit at the iPad viewport and in Chromium. | approved |
| [REQ-5988](REQ-5988-ipad-checklist-source-task.md) | The adult's real-iPad checklist MUST include a Sources track task answered by a tap, a pinch zoom inside the source and a page pinch outside it. | approved |
| [REQ-5990](REQ-5990-first-version-includes-sources-track.md) | The first version MUST include the Sources track «Карта Смотрителя» (the Keeper's Map), with its five nodes and its report screen. | approved |
| [REQ-5992](REQ-5992-track-four-question-levels.md) | Every Sources track task MUST ask at one of four question levels: find, compare or select, calculate from the source, or combine. | approved |
| [REQ-5994](REQ-5994-no-studievaardigheden-named.md) | The report and the Parent Room MUST NOT name a Studievaardigheden test. | approved |
| [REQ-5996](REQ-5996-no-school-test-question.md) | The Parent Room MUST NOT ask the parent which tests the player's school gives. | approved |
| [REQ-6000](REQ-6000-model-ignores-school-snapshots.md) | The knowledge model MUST ignore every school snapshot event, so importing, parsing, correcting or withdrawing a snapshot changes no estimate and no node state. | approved |
| [REQ-6002](REQ-6002-lint-fails-model-reading-school.md) | The lint check MUST fail when code of the knowledge model reads a school snapshot event. | approved |
| [REQ-6004](REQ-6004-snapshot-file-stored-once.md) | The game MUST keep each imported snapshot file exactly once, identified by the hash of its content. | approved |
| [REQ-6006](REQ-6006-snapshot-file-types.md) | The import MUST accept a snapshot as a PDF, PNG, JPEG or WebP file. | approved |
| [REQ-6008](REQ-6008-import-event-fields.md) | When the parent imports a snapshot, the game MUST write one import event carrying the date the document carries, the date of upload, the source the parent names and the file's hash. | approved |
| [REQ-6010](REQ-6010-parent-enters-missing-date.md) | When a snapshot document carries no date, the import MUST take the date from the parent and record in the import event that the parent entered it. | approved |
| [REQ-6012](REQ-6012-reimport-writes-no-copy.md) | When the parent imports a file whose hash the game already keeps, the game MUST NOT store a second copy of it. | approved |
| [REQ-6014](REQ-6014-parse-event-carries-version.md) | Every parse of a snapshot MUST be written as a new event carrying the parser version and the hash of the file it read. | approved |
| [REQ-6016](REQ-6016-new-parser-rereads-old-files.md) | When the parser version changes, the game MUST reread every kept snapshot file that isn't withdrawn into new parse events and leave the earlier parse events unchanged. | approved |
| [REQ-6018](REQ-6018-correction-names-hash-goal-field.md) | A correction the parent makes to a parsed value MUST be written as a new event naming the snapshot's hash, the goal and the field. | approved |
| [REQ-6020](REQ-6020-correction-wins-over-reparse.md) | When a reparse and a correction give different values for the same field, the game MUST use the correction's value. | approved |
| [REQ-6022](REQ-6022-parent-sees-reparse-disagreement.md) | When a reparse and a correction give different values for the same field, the Parent Room MUST show the parent both values and that they disagree. | approved |
| [REQ-6024](REQ-6024-parser-never-infers-fields.md) | The parser MUST leave empty every field the document doesn't show, with no value inferred for it. | approved |
| [REQ-6026](REQ-6026-snapshot-fields-may-be-absent.md) | The import MUST complete for a document that lacks any of these: goal codes, whether a goal is open, the number of tasks per goal, the document's date, the share mastered per subdomain and the level of the material. | approved |
| [REQ-6028](REQ-6028-snapshot-parsed-on-the-mac.md) | The game MUST parse a snapshot on the parent's Mac, for images as well as PDFs, with no part of the file or its text sent off the Mac. | approved |
| [REQ-6030](REQ-6030-no-model-reads-snapshots.md) | A language model MUST NOT read a snapshot file or any value parsed from one. | approved |
| [REQ-6032](REQ-6032-snapshot-changes-rebuilt-by-recompute.md) | The view of changes between snapshots MUST be derived only from the import, parse, correction and withdrawal events, so the full recompute rebuilds it with the same result. | approved |
| [REQ-6034](REQ-6034-target-change-marked-as-such.md) | The view of changes between snapshots MUST mark as a change in the target both a change of target and any change of status between the same two consecutive snapshots as that change of target. | approved |
| [REQ-6036](REQ-6036-school-level-shown-as-national.md) | The "home and school" report MUST present the school's per-goal level as the school's comparison with pupils nationally. | approved |
| [REQ-6038](REQ-6038-school-status-shown-against-target.md) | The "home and school" report MUST present the school's per-goal status as relative to the target the school set for the player. | approved |
| [REQ-6040](REQ-6040-school-and-home-never-merged.md) | The "home and school" report MUST NOT state a school level or status as a home state, or convert a school value into a home state or a home state into a school value. | approved |
| [REQ-6042](REQ-6042-difference-reads-as-two-measures.md) | Where the school's value and the home state for a goal differ, the "home and school" report MUST word the difference as a difference between two measures, not as a fault in the player or in either measure. | approved |
| [REQ-6044](REQ-6044-timeline-separate-series.md) | The timeline MUST draw the school's levels, the Cito results and the home skill scale as separate series, each on its own scale, with no conversion between them. | approved |
| [REQ-6046](REQ-6046-timeline-series-labelled-by-source.md) | The timeline MUST label each series with its source: the school, Cito or the game at home. | approved |
| [REQ-6048](REQ-6048-goal-wording-stays-on-mac.md) | The server MUST NOT send the wording of a school goal to any service outside the home. | approved |
| [REQ-6050](REQ-6050-goals-mapped-by-offline-catalogue.md) | The game MUST propose the nodes for a school goal only from a mapping of the vendor's public goal catalogue kept on the Mac. | approved |
| [REQ-6052](REQ-6052-parent-maps-uncatalogued-goals.md) | The Parent Room MUST let the parent map to nodes by hand any school goal the catalogue mapping lacks. | approved |
| [REQ-6054](REQ-6054-entered-school-goals-map-same-way.md) | School goals the parent enters in the Parent Room MUST map to nodes the same way as goals from a snapshot, through the catalogue mapping or the parent's own mapping, with no model involved. | approved |
| [REQ-6056](REQ-6056-unconfirmed-link-changes-nothing.md) | A link from a school goal to a node MUST change nothing in any report until the parent confirms it. | approved |
| [REQ-6058](REQ-6058-snapshot-link-not-in-director-value.md) | A confirmed link from a school goal in a snapshot MUST NOT add to the value by which the Director ranks nodes. | approved |
| [REQ-6060](REQ-6060-home-and-school-screen.md) | After the MVP, the full report MUST have a "home and school" screen that sets the school's per-goal values beside the home node states of the linked nodes. | approved |
| [REQ-6062](REQ-6062-school-timeline-screen.md) | After the MVP, the full report MUST have a timeline screen showing the school's levels, the Cito results and the home skill scale over time. | approved |
| [REQ-6064](REQ-6064-report-v1-nine-screens-until-mvp-ends.md) | Report v1 MUST have nine screens: the summary, VWO readiness, the graph map, the node card, misconceptions, limits, science, the story book and «Работа с источниками» (Working with sources). | approved |
| [REQ-6066](REQ-6066-school-export-separate.md) | The game MUST offer an export for the school as its own export, apart from the export of the whole event log. | approved |
| [REQ-6068](REQ-6068-school-export-content.md) | The export for the school MUST hold, per snapshot date, the school values as the parser read them, each correction marked as the parent's, and the Cito results the parent entered. | approved |
| [REQ-6070](REQ-6070-school-export-no-home-data.md) | The export for the school MUST NOT hold any home data: no event from play, no estimate, no node state, no free text and no story. | approved |
| [REQ-6072](REQ-6072-school-export-only-on-mac.md) | The game MUST write the export for the school only on the Mac, so a Parent Room opened on another device offers none. | approved |
| [REQ-6074](REQ-6074-import-shows-document-first.md) | Before an import writes any event or file, the Parent Room on the Mac MUST show the parent the document beside the pupil's name the parser read from it, or a notice that it read no name. | approved |
| [REQ-6076](REQ-6076-parent-confirms-document-is-hers.md) | The import MUST write no event and keep no file until the parent confirms the document is about the player. | approved |
| [REQ-6078](REQ-6078-withdrawn-snapshot-hidden.md) | When the parent withdraws a snapshot, the game MUST write a withdrawal event and hide the snapshot and its values from every projection, every report and the export for the school. | approved |
| [REQ-6080](REQ-6080-withdrawn-snapshot-stays-on-mac.md) | A withdrawn snapshot's file and values MUST NOT leave the parent's Mac by any route. | approved |
| [REQ-6082](REQ-6082-no-real-overview-tracked.md) | A real pupil overview, or any part of one, MUST NOT enter a tracked file. | approved |
| [REQ-6084](REQ-6084-school-snapshot-event-types.md) | The event log MUST record snapshots through four event types: `school_snapshot_imported`, `school_snapshot_parsed`, `school_snapshot_corrected` and `school_snapshot_withdrawn`. | approved |
| [REQ-6086](REQ-6086-school-names-vendor-neutral.md) | Event names and code for school snapshots MUST NOT name the vendor of the school's learning system. | approved |
| [REQ-6088](REQ-6088-snapshot-file-never-changed.md) | A kept snapshot file MUST stay byte for byte as it was imported. | approved |
| [REQ-6090](REQ-6090-parser-tests-synthetic-only.md) | Parser tests MUST use only synthetic pupil overviews. | approved |
| [REQ-6092](REQ-6092-no-catalogue-no-proposals.md) | When no public goal catalogue of the vendor exists, the game MUST propose no nodes for a school goal and leave every goal to the parent's own mapping. | approved |
| [REQ-6100](REQ-6100-no-sound-until-parent-turns-it-on.md) | The game MUST play no sound until the parent has turned music or effects on in the Parent Room. | approved |
| [REQ-6102](REQ-6102-new-install-starts-silent.md) | When the game is newly installed or reset, music and effects MUST both be off and set to volume 0. | approved |
| [REQ-6104](REQ-6104-parent-sets-music-and-effects-apart.md) | The Parent Room MUST let the parent turn music and effects on or off separately, each with its own volume. | approved |
| [REQ-6106](REQ-6106-volume-stays-under-ceiling.md) | Every volume the parent sets for music or effects MUST stay at or below a ceiling that keeps the game within the loudness budget of ADR-0150, part 7, and the ADR-0190 Baselines, each file normalised to -20 LUFS and -3 dBTP. | approved |
| [REQ-6108](REQ-6108-player-settings-hold-no-sound.md) | The player's own settings MUST NOT offer any sound control. | approved |
| [REQ-6110](REQ-6110-sound-change-logged-as-parent-setting.md) | When the parent changes music or effects, the event log MUST record the change as a `settings_changed` event and never as a `looks_set` event. | approved |
| [REQ-6112](REQ-6112-silence-leaves-no-audio-trace.md) | While music and effects are both off, the client MUST NOT create an audio context, start playback of any media element or request any sound file. | approved |
| [REQ-6114](REQ-6114-audio-starts-only-on-a-tap.md) | When the parent has turned sound on, the client MUST start audio output only in response to the player's tap. | approved |
| [REQ-6116](REQ-6116-silent-mode-silences-the-game.md) | While the iPad's silent mode is on, the game MUST play no sound, even when the parent has turned music or effects on. | approved |
| [REQ-6118](REQ-6118-every-sound-names-its-event.md) | Every place in the game that plays a sound MUST name the sound event it plays. | approved |
| [REQ-6120](REQ-6120-build-fails-on-sound-without-picture.md) | The build MUST fail when a sound event has no visual equivalent. | approved |
| [REQ-6122](REQ-6122-visual-equivalent-not-colour-alone.md) | A visual equivalent of a sound MUST NOT carry its meaning by colour alone. | approved |
| [REQ-6124](REQ-6124-no-information-by-sound-in-tasks.md) | A task, task template, hint, puzzle or answer form MUST NOT give information by sound. | approved |
| [REQ-6126](REQ-6126-level-up-ceremony-complete-without-sound.md) | In the MVP a level-up MUST show a ceremony of light and motion that is complete with sound off. | approved |
| [REQ-6128](REQ-6128-trick-changes-what-she-sees.md) | A trick MUST change how a spell looks, and leave everything but its look and sound unchanged, its power included. | approved |
| [REQ-6130](REQ-6130-heard-canon-has-a-seen-sign.md) | Every creature, place or clue that the canon defines by what the heroine hears MUST also have a sign the player sees, starting with Whisperkin, the Music Box and the humming lamps and carousel of the dreamcore floors. | approved |
| [REQ-6132](REQ-6132-eye-exercise-lasts-at-least-half-minute.md) | An eye exercise MUST last at least 30 seconds, and at most 40 seconds when it keeps the player's eyes on the screen. | approved |
| [REQ-6134](REQ-6134-eyes-off-exercise-ends-by-her-tap.md) | When an eye exercise takes the player's eyes off the screen, that is looking far out of the window or covering the eyes with the palms, the exercise MUST end only when she taps «Готово» (Done). | approved |
| [REQ-6136](REQ-6136-eyes-off-done-button-waits-half-minute.md) | In an eye exercise that takes the player's eyes off the screen, the «Готово» (Done) button MUST stay inactive for the first 30 seconds and show no countdown. | approved |
| [REQ-6138](REQ-6138-familiar-gives-length-in-her-terms.md) | When an eye exercise takes the player's eyes off the screen, the familiar MUST tell her how long to do it in a measure she keeps herself, such as slow breaths, with no number, minutes or seconds in the line. | approved |
| [REQ-6140](REQ-6140-story-needs-no-voice.md) | Every story choice MUST be possible by tapping a suggestion or by typing, without using voice input. | approved |
| [REQ-6142](REQ-6142-tap-shows-within-100-ms.md) | Every tap MUST change the tapped control's look within 100 ms. | approved |
| [REQ-6144](REQ-6144-task-window-press-is-instant.md) | Inside the task window, a tapped control MUST change its look as an instant change of state, with no transition. | approved |
| [REQ-6146](REQ-6146-familiar-plays-while-master-writes.md) | While the player waits for the Master's reply, the familiar MUST do something funny in the scene column. | approved |
| [REQ-6148](REQ-6148-no-time-sign-while-master-writes.md) | While the player waits for the Master's reply, the game MUST NOT show a spinner, a progress bar or any other sign of time passing or left. | approved |
| [REQ-6150](REQ-6150-first-line-within-two-seconds.md) | After the player sends free text, the 95th percentile of the time until the first character of a reply line shows MUST be at most 2 seconds. | approved |
| [REQ-6152](REQ-6152-early-line-comes-from-checked-bank.md) | Every line that shows before the Master's reply to the player's free text MUST come from a bank of lines that passed, before play, the Master-reply checks REQ-1616 names and the forbidden-list check of REQ-3326. | approved |
| [REQ-6154](REQ-6154-reaction-line-not-repeated-in-adventure.md) | A prepared reaction line MUST NOT show again in an adventure while the bank still holds a line not yet shown in that adventure. | approved |
| [REQ-6156](REQ-6156-silent-acceptance-test.md) | The silent-play acceptance test MUST pass only when the Awakening and one adventure of the day, played on WebKit with default settings, reach their end with no audio context created, no media element played and no sound file requested. | approved |
| [REQ-6158](REQ-6158-spike-shows-new-install-silent.md) | The stage 0 spike MUST show on a real iPad that a new install plays no sound. | approved |
| [REQ-6160](REQ-6160-spike-shows-parent-switches-apart.md) | The stage 0 spike MUST show on a real iPad that the parent's switches turn music and effects on separately. | approved |
| [REQ-6162](REQ-6162-spike-shows-silent-mode-holds.md) | The stage 0 spike MUST show on a real iPad that silent mode silences music and effects the parent has turned on. | approved |
| [REQ-6164](REQ-6164-visual-equivalent-stands-alone.md) | Every sound event's visual equivalent MUST convey the same information as its sound on its own, with sound off. | approved |
| [REQ-6200](REQ-6200-two-free-action-points.md) | Each adventure MUST offer at least 2 free-action points, at least one of which isn't the lead-in to a trial, where what the player sends is kept as a story fact that a later scene reads. | approved |
| [REQ-6202](REQ-6202-free-action-consequence-next-adventure.md) | When the player sends a free action that isn't an unchanged starter, a later scene of the same adventure or the next one MUST show her a consequence of it. | approved |
| [REQ-6204](REQ-6204-taskless-scene-each-adventure.md) | Each adventure MUST hold at least one scene without tasks that is neither the lead-in to a trial nor a transition. | approved |
| [REQ-6206](REQ-6206-report-shows-task-window-share.md) | The report MUST show, for each adventure, the share of the adventure's active time during which the task window was open, against a target of 60 %. | approved |
| [REQ-6208](REQ-6208-scene-offers-starters-and-next.md) | Every scene MUST offer three starters and a «Дальше» (Next) button that moves the story on while the text field is empty. | approved |
| [REQ-6210](REQ-6210-starter-tap-inserts-only.md) | When the player taps a starter, the game MUST insert its words into the text field without sending them. | approved |
| [REQ-6212](REQ-6212-keys-insert-starter.md) | When the player presses 1, 2 or 3 on a physical keyboard while the story input's text field is empty, the game MUST insert the matching starter's words into the field without sending them. | approved |
| [REQ-6214](REQ-6214-one-strange-starter.md) | One of every scene's three starters MUST be strange and funny. | approved |
| [REQ-6216](REQ-6216-free-action-in-her-own-words.md) | At least one of each adventure's free-action points MUST count as the player's own action only text she typed, dictated or edited, and never a starter she sent unchanged. | approved |
| [REQ-6218](REQ-6218-sending-options-only-for-plot-choices.md) | Ready-made options that send on a tap MUST appear only where the plot itself offers a choice, such as the route, a name or where to go. | approved |
| [REQ-6220](REQ-6220-free-pen-opens-outside-task-window.md) | Once the free pen has opened, and unless the parent has switched it off, the game MUST let the player open the free pen at any moment the task window isn't open, during an adventure included. | approved |
| [REQ-6221](REQ-6221-free-pen-passes-free-text-checks.md) | Text the player writes in the free pen MUST pass the same triggers, judge and checks, and carry the same parent note, as every other free text she writes. | approved |
| [REQ-6222](REQ-6222-free-pen-no-task-or-score.md) | The free pen MUST NOT give a task, a score or any knowledge-model event. | approved |
| [REQ-6224](REQ-6224-parent-switches-free-pen-off.md) | The Parent Room MUST offer a setting that switches the free pen off, on until the parent changes it. | approved |
| [REQ-6226](REQ-6226-free-pen-counts-in-active-time.md) | Time the player spends in the free pen MUST count in the day's active time. | approved |
| [REQ-6228](REQ-6228-free-pen-closes-through-story.md) | When the day's active time passes the soft-stop point or the day's adventure budget runs out while the free pen is open, the free pen MUST close the book through a story scene and offer no extension. | approved |
| [REQ-6230](REQ-6230-free-pen-spends-adventure-budget.md) | The free pen's model calls MUST spend only what the day's adventure budget left, with no budget of their own. | approved |
| [REQ-6232](REQ-6232-return-lines-silent-on-gap.md) | The first scene after at least one game day on which the player didn't play MUST NOT mention the gap or her absence, say that anyone missed her or waited for her, or ask where she was. | approved |
| [REQ-6233](REQ-6233-return-phrase-list-parent-approved.md) | The game MUST refuse any return line that holds a phrase from a list, approved by the parent, of phrases that miss the player, wait for her or ask where she was. | approved |
| [REQ-6234](REQ-6234-adventure-order-with-route-choice.md) | The adventure of the day MUST run in this order: «В прошлый раз…» (Last time…) with the daily quests once their system has opened, then a story choice between two routes, then 3 maths floors, or 4 when the forecast leaves time, then a finale that ends on a cliffhanger. | approved |
| [REQ-6236](REQ-6236-both-routes-keep-window.md) | Both routes the adventure offers MUST keep what a single route measures: the three-day domain window, the rule that an overdue domain comes first and the same planned number of graded slots. | approved |
| [REQ-6238](REQ-6238-routes-differ-where-window-allows.md) | The two routes MUST differ in at least one floor where the three-day domain window allows it, and otherwise in floor order and story. | approved |
| [REQ-6240](REQ-6240-next-takes-higher-value-route.md) | When the player presses «Дальше» (Next) at the choice of routes, the game MUST take the route of higher total value by the Director's ranking. | approved |
| [REQ-6242](REQ-6242-memo-first-week-checklist.md) | The Parent Room's memo MUST hold a checklist for the player's first week of play. | approved |
| [REQ-6243](REQ-6243-session-zero-trains-no-threads.md) | Session 0 MUST NOT train the player on guiding threads, hints or explanations. | approved |
| [REQ-6244](REQ-6244-systems-open-on-schedule.md) | Unless the parent has opened every system at once, the game MUST open new systems by days of play on this schedule: levels and the chest on day 1; guiding threads, the hint ladder and the volley on day 2; daily quests and the first knot on day 3; the Diary and the bestiary on day 4; a second familiar on day 5; the forge on day 6; the shop, the free pen and the Tower's dictionary on day 8; evolution and the Underside when their events come. | approved |
| [REQ-6245](REQ-6245-short-solution-before-threads-open.md) | Before guiding threads have opened, the game MUST still show the free short solution after a wrong answer. | approved |
| [REQ-6246](REQ-6246-parent-opens-all-systems.md) | The Parent Room MUST offer a setting that opens every system at once. | approved |
| [REQ-6248](REQ-6248-threads-each-game-day-once-open.md) | At the start of each game day after guiding threads have opened, the game MUST add 3 guiding threads to the player's stock. | approved |
| [REQ-6250](REQ-6250-thread-button-once-threads-open.md) | Once guiding threads have opened, the task window MUST always show the thread button with the player's current number of guiding threads. | approved |
| [REQ-6252](REQ-6252-chest-diary-pages-once-diary-open.md) | Every chest MUST offer three rewards from three different categories, drawn from cosmetics; star-steel shards and star yarn, which form one category; buttons; and, once the Diary has opened, Diary pages. | approved |
| [REQ-6254](REQ-6254-master-avoids-overused-phrases.md) | When a sequence of 3 to 5 words, counted by lemma so Russian inflections match, has appeared more than 3 times in the Master's text over the last 14 days, the game MUST tell the Master not to use it in the scenes that follow. | approved |
| [REQ-6256](REQ-6256-repeating-scene-regenerated-once.md) | When a drafted scene that isn't a reply to the player's free text still holds a phrase the Master was told not to use, the game MUST regenerate it once. | approved |
| [REQ-6258](REQ-6258-openings-dealt-from-decks.md) | The Master's openings, finales and character temperaments MUST come from decks dealt without replacement. | approved |
| [REQ-6260](REQ-6260-opening-similarity-without-model.md) | The similarity of each opening to the last 14 openings MUST be scored on the Mac with no model call. | approved |
| [REQ-6262](REQ-6262-weekly-random-scenes-reviewed.md) | Each week, 5 of the Master's scenes drawn at random MUST enter the parent's review queue. | approved |
| [REQ-6264](REQ-6264-freshness-spares-whitelist.md) | The check on repeated phrases MUST NOT strike the running jokes in story memory, the canon's catchphrases or the names the player gave. | approved |
| [REQ-6266](REQ-6266-known-character-each-adventure.md) | In each adventure the Director MUST bring into a scene at least one character from the canon's recurring cast whom the player has already met. | approved |
| [REQ-6268](REQ-6268-reopen-past-chapter-or-scene.md) | The player MUST be able to reread any past chapter and rewatch any past scene with no model call. | approved |
| [REQ-6270](REQ-6270-mark-scene-as-favourite.md) | The player MUST be able to mark a scene as a favourite. | approved |
| [REQ-6272](REQ-6272-hidden-detail-keeps-later-secrets.md) | A hidden detail MUST NOT reveal a secret of a checkpoint the player hasn't reached. | approved |
| [REQ-6274](REQ-6274-hidden-detail-never-needed.md) | The player MUST be able to progress through the story without finding any hidden detail. | approved |
| [REQ-6276](REQ-6276-rituals-follow-game-events.md) | Every ritual MUST follow a game event, such as a floor's first scene, a chest or the finale, and never a time of day or a calendar day. | approved |
| [REQ-6278](REQ-6278-master-refuses-only-for-safety.md) | The Master MUST refuse a story action only when the safety checklist or a trigger requires it. | approved |
| [REQ-6279](REQ-6279-refusal-offers-story-turn.md) | When the Master refuses a story action, it MUST offer the player a turn in the story in the same reply. | approved |
| [REQ-6280](REQ-6280-trial-cancelling-action-redirected.md) | When the player's action would cancel a trial, the Master MUST answer with a "yes, and" redirection that keeps the trial. | approved |
| [REQ-6282](REQ-6282-name-refusal-names-limit.md) | When the game refuses a name the player gives, it MUST say which limit the name broke and offer a way on. | approved |
| [REQ-6284](REQ-6284-show-card-for-parent-room.md) | A "Show" card the player makes from a scene or a moment MUST reach only the Parent Room behind the PIN. | approved |
| [REQ-6286](REQ-6286-story-book-pdf-in-mvp.md) | In the MVP, the parent MUST be able to export the story book as a PDF. | approved |
| [REQ-6288](REQ-6288-interest-section-contents.md) | The report MUST hold an "Interest" section, built from the event log and the parent's daily mark, that shows whether the player came back by herself, her extensions, the length of her own text and its share of what she sends, the optional activities and the systems she used, and a figure for how often the Master repeats itself. | approved |
| [REQ-6290](REQ-6290-parent-marks-came-back-alone.md) | The Parent Room MUST let the parent mark, for each day, whether the player came back to the game by herself. | approved |
| [REQ-6292](REQ-6292-signal-story-stops-being-hers.md) | When the player's own words per adventure, not counting starter text, fall by 30 % or more between the last 14 days of play and the 14 before, and the share of her sends that are an unchanged starter rises over the same windows, the report MUST raise the signal "the story is stopping being hers". | approved |
| [REQ-6294](REQ-6294-backlog-waits-on-quiet-signal.md) | A week of play MUST count towards a backlog item's acceptance only when the signal "the story is stopping being hers" wasn't raised during that week. | approved |
| [REQ-6296](REQ-6296-sixty-day-interest-acceptance.md) | Before acceptance, a simulated run of 60 days MUST show at least 2 free-action points and one scene without tasks in every adventure, systems opening on schedule, starters that only insert, no task in the free pen, return lines free of the refused phrases, the interest signal firing on synthetic data, no whitelisted phrase struck, and a known character in every adventure. | approved |
| [REQ-6298](REQ-6298-log-holds-engagement-events.md) | The event log MUST hold the event types `system_unlocked`, `route_offered`, `route_chosen`, `free_pen_started`, `free_pen_ended`, `starter_inserted`, `share_card_created`, `share_card_viewed`, `scene_rewatched`, `chapter_reread`, `scene_favorited`, `hidden_detail_found`, `parent_day_marked` and `text_freshness_scored`. | approved |
| [REQ-6300](REQ-6300-sandbox-own-database.md) | The sandbox MUST keep its game in a database of its own, separate from the player's database. | approved |
| [REQ-6302](REQ-6302-sandbox-database-beside-live-one.md) | The sandbox's database MUST live in the same container storage as the player's live database, and never in the folder shared between the Mac and the container. | approved |
| [REQ-6304](REQ-6304-sandbox-log-append-only.md) | The sandbox's event log MUST refuse to change or delete an event, as the player's log does. | approved |
| [REQ-6306](REQ-6306-sandbox-reads-player-database-read-only.md) | The sandbox MUST read the player's database only through a connection that can't write to it. | approved |
| [REQ-6308](REQ-6308-sandbox-opening-changes-nothing.md) | Opening the player's database for the sandbox MUST change no table and no row in it. | approved |
| [REQ-6310](REQ-6310-sandbox-events-marked.md) | Every event written to the sandbox's own database MUST carry a mark that tells it apart from the player's events. | approved |
| [REQ-6312](REQ-6312-player-database-refuses-sandbox-events.md) | The player's database MUST itself refuse to store an event that carries the sandbox mark, whatever code sends it. | approved |
| [REQ-6314](REQ-6314-server-refuses-start-without-refusal.md) | When the player's database lacks its refusal of sandbox-marked events, the server MUST refuse to start. | approved |
| [REQ-6316](REQ-6316-migration-keeps-sandbox-refusal.md) | The migration runner MUST refuse a migration that removes or replaces the player's database's refusal of sandbox-marked events. | approved |
| [REQ-6318](REQ-6318-log-writer-refuses-sandbox-events.md) | The game's log writer MUST refuse a sandbox-marked event bound for the player's database before the database sees it. | approved |
| [REQ-6320](REQ-6320-sandbox-snapshot-one-point-in-time.md) | A snapshot of the player's state for the sandbox MUST show that state as it stood at one point in time, even while she plays. | approved |
| [REQ-6322](REQ-6322-sandbox-copy-holds-no-credential.md) | The sandbox's database MUST hold no credential copied from the player's database, where the credentials are the PIN hash, the PIN lockout records and the device tokens and their hashes. | approved |
| [REQ-6324](REQ-6324-sandbox-reset-leaves-player-database.md) | When the parent resets the sandbox, the reset MUST leave the player's database unchanged. | approved |
| [REQ-6326](REQ-6326-sandbox-reset-offers-two-starts.md) | When the parent resets the sandbox, the game MUST offer to start it from an empty profile or from a copy of the last sandbox snapshot of the player's state. | approved |
| [REQ-6328](REQ-6328-sandbox-model-calls-logged-in-sandbox.md) | Every model call the sandbox makes MUST be recorded, both its request-and-response record and its call event, in the sandbox's database and never in the player's. | approved |
| [REQ-6330](REQ-6330-sandbox-model-calls-pass-play-guards.md) | Every model call the sandbox makes MUST pass the same checks on what leaves the Mac, at the same privacy level, as a call by the same role in play. | approved |
| [REQ-6336](REQ-6336-play-count-leaves-out-sandbox.md) | The game's count of the play key's monthly spend MUST leave out every model call the sandbox makes, from the Parent Room or from the command line. | approved |
| [REQ-6340](REQ-6340-cost-report-shows-sandbox-spend.md) | The cost report MUST show the sandbox's spend on a line of its own, against the sandbox's monthly cap. | approved |
| [REQ-6342](REQ-6342-sandbox-needs-parent-session.md) | Every sandbox request that reaches the game's network listener MUST require a parent session opened with the PIN on the device making the request. | approved |
| [REQ-6344](REQ-6344-sandbox-requests-count-as-parent-activity.md) | Every sandbox request, including one shaped like a play request, MUST count as parent activity for the parent session's idle expiry. | approved |
| [REQ-6346](REQ-6346-sandbox-screens-marked.md) | Every sandbox screen MUST show a striped frame with the label «Песочница — не влияет на игру» (Sandbox: does not affect the game). | approved |
| [REQ-6348](REQ-6348-sandbox-changes-only-by-confirmed-actions.md) | The sandbox MUST NOT change the player's record, except through the confirmed actions: marking a task ambiguous, disabling a template or a puzzle, restoring one that was disabled, and approving a puzzle or a variant. | approved |
| [REQ-6350](REQ-6350-confirmed-action-needs-second-press.md) | When the parent chooses a confirmed action in the sandbox, the game MUST apply it only after a second, confirming press. | approved |
| [REQ-6352](REQ-6352-confirmed-action-logged-from-sandbox.md) | Each confirmation of a confirmed action MUST enter the player's log as exactly one event, without the sandbox mark and naming the sandbox as its source. | approved |
| [REQ-6354](REQ-6354-disabled-item-survives-rebuild.md) | A template or a puzzle disabled or restored from the sandbox MUST stay as the parent left it for the player after the server restarts and after derived data are rebuilt from the log. | approved |
| [REQ-6356](REQ-6356-old-parent-events-still-read.md) | Every parent event written before parent events gained a source MUST still be read, with the same meaning, after they gain it. | approved |
| [REQ-6358](REQ-6358-calibration-outside-sandbox.md) | The adult's calibration of a device type's fluency threshold MUST run in the Parent Room and never in the sandbox. | approved |
| [REQ-6360](REQ-6360-command-line-sandbox.md) | The Mac MUST offer a command-line sandbox, as `./meowtower sandbox item`, `./meowtower sandbox batch` and `./meowtower sandbox adventure --from-snapshot`, each printing its result as JSON: `item` runs one task item, `batch` runs a batch of 10 to 50 task items, and `adventure --from-snapshot` plays an adventure as the player from the last sandbox snapshot of her state. | approved |
| [REQ-6362](REQ-6362-command-line-sandbox-mac-only.md) | The command-line sandbox MUST answer only requests made on the Mac itself. | approved |
| [REQ-6364](REQ-6364-command-line-sandbox-applies-no-action.md) | The server's listener for the Mac's own commands, which asks no PIN, MUST offer no way to apply a confirmed action. | approved |
| [REQ-6366](REQ-6366-snapshot-output-never-tracked.md) | The command-line sandbox MUST print its output and write it to no file. | approved |
| [REQ-6368](REQ-6368-repository-scan-finds-snapshot-output.md) | The repository's scan for personal data MUST fail when a tracked file holds output of a sandbox run on a snapshot of the player's state, recognised both by the mark REQ-6372 puts in that output and by the personal values the scan already looks for. | approved |
| [REQ-6370](REQ-6370-sandbox-scenario-leaves-player-database.md) | A sandbox scenario with no confirmed action MUST leave every table of the player's database unchanged, from just after the parent's PIN login to the scenario's end. | approved |
| [REQ-6372](REQ-6372-command-line-output-marked.md) | Every output of the command-line sandbox MUST carry a mark that shows it came from a sandbox run. | approved |
| [REQ-6374](REQ-6374-sandbox-spend-survives-reset.md) | The sandbox's spend in the current month MUST still count toward its cost-report line and its monthly cap after the parent resets the sandbox. | approved |
| [REQ-6376](REQ-6376-parent-session-activity-outside-player-database.md) | The parent session's activity MUST NOT be recorded in the player's database. | approved |
| [REQ-6378](REQ-6378-reset-copy-takes-missing-snapshot.md) | When the parent chooses the copy at a sandbox reset and no sandbox snapshot of the player's state exists yet, the game MUST take one and start the sandbox from it. | approved |
